  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 暗物质-电子散射率的计算通常需要详细的电子结构信息，这限制了对不同凝聚态探测器材料的普适性理解。作者希望找到一种不依赖具体材料细节的方法来约束散射率。

Method: 利用电子能量损失函数的第一性原理求和规则，推导暗物质-电子散射率的上界。该上界仅需三个宏观材料参数：等离子体频率ω_p、质量密度ρ_T和有限动量转移下的静态纵向介电函数ε(q,0)。对于介电函数的大q行为，作者提出了仅依赖ω_p的简单标度律，并用解析和数值例子加以验证。

Result: 作者验证了ε(q,0)的大q依赖关系可被仅依赖ω_p的标度律近似描述，而ω_p和ρ_T在广泛材料中变化范围有限，因此所得上界在很大程度上与具体材料无关。

Conclusion: 该上界为任何探测暗物质与电子密度耦合的直接探测实验的灵敏度设定了根本性极限，具有广泛的普适性。

Abstract: A wide variety of condensed matter systems are used or proposed as detectors to search for dark matter-electron scattering. In general, the scattering rate depends on detailed knowledge of the electronic properties of these systems. However, when dark matter couples to electron density, the dark matter-electron scattering rate can be related to the electron energy loss function, whose integrals are bounded by first-principles sum rules that rely on only a few macroscopic target properties. In this paper, we use these first-principles sum rules to derive upper bounds on the dark matter-electron scattering rate depending on only a few material properties: the plasma frequency $ω_\text{p}$, the target mass density $ρ_T$, and the static (longitudinal) dielectric function at finite momentum transfer, $\varepsilon(q, 0)$. The bulk material properties $ω_\text{p}$ and $ρ_T$ vary only over a limited range across a wide variety of materials, and to a good approximation, the generic large-$q$ dependence of $\varepsilon(q, 0)$ can be understood from a simple scaling law depending only on $ω_\text{p}$ which we verify with analytic and numerical examples. Thus, our upper bounds are largely material-agnostic, and place a fundamental limit on the sensitivity of any dark matter-electron direct detection experiment probing the coupling to electron density.

</details>


### [98] [Axion-driven spontaneous leptogenesis, precisely](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05279)
*Konstantin Kuckenberg,Martin A. Mojahed,Kai Schmitz,Hidenaga Watanabe*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文重新审视了一种最小化的自发轻子生成（spontaneous leptogenesis）场景，其中不对称性由通过标准错位机制演化的重轴子场驱动。作者首次给出了Weinberg算符的完全味依赖相互作用率，推导了一个适用于任意轴子速度和经典shift对称耦合的代数主公式，并评估了不同轴子耦合的宇宙学可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索自发轻子生成的最小化场景，其中轻子数破坏源于五维Weinberg算符，而标准热轻子生成的贡献可忽略。需要完整理解味依赖相互作用率，并评估轴子场在不同耦合下的宇宙学可行性，以确定能否成功解释观测到的重子不对称性。

Method: 通过求解一组输运方程计算不对称性，其中首次引入完全味依赖的Weinberg算符相互作用率。作者还推导了一个简化的代数主公式，适用于任意轴子速度和经典shift对称耦合。此外，定量评估了低能中微子质量和两种质量排序对最终不对称性预测的影响，并通过考虑末态轴子衰变引起的熵稀释和重子等曲率扰动约束来检验不同轴子耦合的宇宙学可行性。

Result: 标准Majoron错位机制解释重子不对称性面临严重张力，因为末态衰变导致的熵稀释效应。而与强或弱sphaleron耦合的轴子可以在衰变常数接近大统一能标、轴子振荡温度T_osc≥10^{11-12} GeV的条件下成功产生观测不对称性，前提是需要高标度暴胀和高效再加热的宇宙学历史。

Conclusion: 自发轻子生成中，轴子与sphaleron耦合的方案比标准Majoron错位更可行，但需要较高的能量标度和特定的宇宙学历史条件才能成功解释观测到的重子不对称性。

Abstract: We revisit a minimal scenario for spontaneous leptogenesis where the asymmetry generation is driven by a heavy axion-like field evolving via standard misalignment. We focus on the simplest framework in which lepton number violation originates from the dimension-five Weinberg operator and contributions from standard thermal leptogenesis become negligible. The asymmetry is computed by solving a set of transport equations, which incorporate fully flavor-dependent interaction rates for the Weinberg operator that are presented here for the first time. We also derive a simple algebraic master formula for the generated $B\!-\!L$ asymmetry, valid for arbitrary axion velocities and classically shift-symmetric couplings to the Standard Model, that reproduces the $B\!-\!L$-production dynamics of the full set of transport equations in controlled parameter regions. Along the way, we quantify the importance of low-scale neutrino masses and the two mass orderings on the final prediction for the generated asymmetry. Finally, we assess the cosmological viability of different axion couplings by accounting for entropy dilution from late-time axion decays and imposing constraints from baryonic isocurvature perturbations. We demonstrate that explaining the baryon asymmetry via standard Majoron misalignment is under severe tension due to dilution from late-time decays. In contrast, axions coupled to strong or weak sphalerons can successfully produce the observed asymmetry for decay constants near the energy scale of grand unification and axion oscillation temperatures $T_{\text{osc}} \gtrsim 10^{11\cdots12}$ GeV, provided a cosmological history featuring high-scale inflation and efficient reheating.

</details>


### [99] [Novel Signatures of Matter-Induced Dark Matter Decay in Large-Volume Neutrino Telescopes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05284)
*Hooman Davoudiasl,Dan Hooper,Samyak Jain*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出利用大型中微子望远镜搜索暗物质衰变产生的双缪子事件。作者考虑了一种激发态暗物质在真空中极长寿命、但在物质环境中衰变显著加快的机制，提出了两种实现方案，并计算了缪子能量和开角的分布，表明可行的参数空间可在IceCube、KM3NeT等望远镜中产生可观测的信号。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统暗物质湮灭或衰变受到严格约束，无法在大型中微子望远镜中产生可观测率的双缪子事件（一对高能、高度非准直、来自同一顶点的缪子径迹）。因此需要新的物理机制来产生这种具有零标准模型本底的显著信号。

Method: 提出两种物质增强暗物质衰变的实现机制：(1) 普通物质源的长程标量场修改暗区质量谱，在近地环境中运动学上打开χ2→χ1Z'衰变；(2) 标量背景诱导重Z'与光子之间的动力学混合，在富含物质的环境中大增强三体衰变χ2→χ1μ+μ-。据此计算缪子能量分布和开角分布，并评估事件率。

Result: 计算结果表明，可行的参数空间可以在IceCube、KM3NeT等大型中微子望远镜中产生可观测的事件率，同时与现有实验约束保持一致。论文还初步讨论了IceCube对质量≥1 TeV的宇宙学长寿命带电粒子衰变产生的多缪子事件的灵敏度。

Conclusion: 物质增强的激发态暗物质衰变是一种新颖的、可在大型中微子望远镜中观测到的暗物质信号。双缪子事件具有极低的标准模型本底，是探索暗物质新物理的有力探针，值得在现有和未来的中微子望远镜中进行专门搜索。

Abstract: Large-volume neutrino telescopes offer a unique opportunity to search for decaying dark matter through events containing a pair of energetic, highly non-collimated muon tracks emerging from a common vertex. Such events would have negligible Standard Model backgrounds and would constitute a striking signature of new physics. Conventional dark matter annihilation or decay, however, is too strongly constrained to produce an observable rate of such events. We therefore consider scenarios in which an excited dark matter state is extremely long-lived in vacuum but decays much more rapidly in the presence of ordinary matter. We present two realizations of this mechanism. In the first, a long-range scalar field sourced by ordinary matter modifies the dark-sector mass spectrum, kinematically opening the decay $χ_2 \rightarrow χ_1 Z'$ near the Earth while leaving it forbidden in vacuum. In the second, the scalar background induces kinetic mixing between a heavy $Z'$ and the photon, greatly enhancing the three-body decay $χ_2\toχ_1μ^+μ^-$ in matter-rich environments. We calculate the resulting distributions of muon energies and opening angles and show that viable regions of parameter space can yield observable event rates in IceCube, KM3NeT, and other large-volume neutrino telescopes while remaining consistent with existing constraints. We also briefly consider the sensitivity of IceCube to multi-muon events produced by the decays of cosmologically long-lived charged particles with masses $\gtrsim 1$ TeV.

</details>


### [100] [GOOFy-compatible 3HDMs and beyond](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05304)
*I. de Medeiros Varzielas,A. Kunčinas*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了多Higgs模型中的广义非标准"GOOFy"变换，开发了一种符号轨道（sign-orbit）技术来分类这些变换下的二次型（双线性）结构，并证明非零GOOFy相容二次扇区存在的充要条件，进而揭示SU(2)的特殊代数性质是r0→-r0流形重整化群稳定性的根源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统Higgs族变换和一般CP变换已被广泛研究，但更广义的非标准"GOOFy"变换（标量场与共轭场被赋予相关但不等价的变换）对势能二次扇区的影响尚未被系统分类，因此需要一种系统性的方法来解决这一空白。

Method: 开发了一种符号轨道（sign-orbit）技术，用于确定与GOOFy变换相容的二次型结构。该方法应用于三Higgs二重态模型，并形式化地推广到更大的多Higgs扇区。

Result: 证明了非零GOOFy相容二次扇区存在需要两个独立条件同时满足：底层群承认非平凡符号特征（non-trivial sign character），且该特征在标量表示与其共轭的张量积中被实现。关键结论是r0→-r0流形在孤立情况下的重整化群稳定性并非多Higgs势的一般性质，而是SU(2)特殊代数性质的直接结果。

Conclusion: 该研究提供了GOOFy二次结构及其不变双线性子空间的完整分类，揭示了它们重整化群稳定性的代数障碍，加深了对多Higgs势中非标准对称性结构的理解。

Abstract: Beyond conventional Higgs-family and general CP transformations, one may also consider a broader class of non-standard "GOOFy" transformations, in which the scalar fields and their conjugates are assigned related but inequivalent transformations. Although these transformations are not symmetries of a full Lagrangian in the conventional sense, some nevertheless stabilise the scalar potential. Their impact on the quadratic sector has not yet been systematically classified. We develop a sign-orbit technique to determine the bilinear structures compatible with these transformations. Applied to three-Higgs-doublet models, and formulated so as to extend to larger multi-Higgs sectors, the method shows that a non-vanishing GOOFy-compatible quadratic sector exists only when two independent conditions are met: the underlying group admits a non-trivial sign character, and that character is realised within the tensor product of the scalar representation and its conjugate. As a key consequence, we demonstrate that the renormalisation-group stability of the $r_0 \to -r_0$ manifold in isolation is not a generic feature of multi-Higgs potentials, but rather a direct consequence of special algebraic properties of $SU(2)$. These results provide a classification of the admissible GOOFy quadratic structures and their invariant bilinear subspaces, highlighting the algebraic obstructions to their renormalisation-group stability.

</details>


### [101] [Beyond Borel Windows: A Systematic Optimization Framework for QCD Sum Rules](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05322)
*Raphael M. Albuquerque*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种系统化的QCD Laplace求和规则中求和窗口（sum rule window）确定框架，结合OPE熵准则、局域质量平稳性和辅助参数关联分析，取代传统固定收敛百分比和视觉平台选择方法。应用于全粲四夸克态J^{PC}=2^{++}的研究，预测质量M=(6.32±0.11) GeV，与ATLAS和CMS观测到的近阈值共振结构兼容。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的QCD求和规则中，求和窗口（sum rule window）的确定依赖于固定收敛百分比和视觉平台选择，这种方法缺乏系统性和可复现性，且无法客观量化辅助参数（如Laplace参数τ、连续阈tc、流混合角θ）敏感度对最终质量预测的影响。

Method: 提出三步优化的系统化框架：(1)引入归一化OPE熵量化QCD微扰与非微扰贡献的重新分布，划定初始工作区（Working Region）；(2)通过最小化质量估计量的残差变化来细化该区域；(3)要求对连续阈值和流组成具有弱敏感性。同时引入Laplace参数τ、连续阈tc和插值流混合角θ的关联分析。应用于混合diquark-antidiquark与meson-meson插值流的全粲四夸克态2^{++}研究。

Result: 优化得到θ=17.2°±0.4°，√t_c=7.08±0.08 GeV，τ=0.410±0.010 GeV^{-2}，质量预测M=(6.32±0.11) GeV。该态位于di-J/ψ谱的近阈值区域，与ATLAS报道的最低共振成分及CMS中参数化为BW_0的增强在不确定度内兼容。

Conclusion: 该框架提供了一种可复现的方式来确定辅助参数敏感度较低的有限域，并将剩余的参数依赖性纳入最终不确定度中，为QCD求和规则分析提供了更严谨和系统化的方法论。

Abstract: We propose a systematic optimization framework for determining the sum rule window in QCD Laplace sum rules. Instead of relying primarily on fixed convergence percentages and visual plateau selection, the procedure combines an OPE entropy criterion, local mass stationarity, and a correlated analysis of the Laplace parameter $τ$, the continuum threshold $t_c$, and the interpolating-current mixing angle $θ$. The normalized OPE entropy is introduced to quantify the redistribution of the QCD contributions between the perturbative and nonperturbative sectors and to delimit an initial Working Region. This domain is subsequently refined by minimizing the residual variation of the mass estimator and requiring weak sensitivity to the continuum threshold and to the current composition. As an application, we study the lowest fully charmed tetraquark state with quantum numbers $J^{PC}=2^{++}$ using a mixed diquark--antidiquark and meson--meson interpolating current. The optimization selects $θ=17.2^\circ\pm0.4^\circ$, $\sqrt{t_c}=7.08\pm0.08~\mathrm{GeV}$, and $τ=0.410\pm0.010~\mathrm{GeV}^{-2}$, leading to the mass prediction $M_{c\bar c c\bar c}^{2^{++}}=(6.32\pm0.11)~\mathrm{GeV}$. The predicted state lies in the near-threshold region of the di-$J/ψ$ spectrum and is compatible, within uncertainties, with the lowest resonant component reported by ATLAS and with the enhancement parametrized as $BW_0$ in the recent CMS publication. The proposed framework provides a reproducible way of identifying finite domains of reduced auxiliary-parameter sensitivity and of incorporating the remaining dependence into the final uncertainty.

</details>


### [102] [Photon-nucleon entanglement in Compton scattering at low and high energies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05330)
*Yoshitaka Hatta,Víctor Martínez-Fernández*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究康普顿散射中末态光子-核子系统的自旋-自旋纠缠，涵盖低能（π阈值以下）和高能（微扰QCD直到次领头阶）两个区域。作者首先证明了无极化康普顿散射中，若散射振幅为实数则无法产生纠缠的no-go定理；随后考虑极化康普顿散射，发现电子、质子和中子靶在低能下展现出丰富的最大纠缠Bell态，且质子与中子的纠缠模式存在显著差异，中子靶的电、磁极化率会强烈影响甚至决定纠缠是否存在。这表明纠缠可作为研究核子电磁性质的新工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索量子纠缠作为研究核子电磁性质（特别是极化率）的新型探针，从量子信息角度理解康普顿散射这一基础过程，并揭示不同靶粒子（电子、质子、中子）在纠缠特性上的差异。

Method: 理论分析与解析计算结合。先在一般性条件下证明no-go定理（实数散射振幅下无纠缠）；随后对低能区域（低于π介子阈值）和高能区域（微扰QCD到次领头阶）分别研究极化康普顿散射，系统分析电子、质子、中子三种靶粒子在不同运动学区域产生的纠缠态结构，包括最大纠缠Bell态及其幺正等价形式。

Result: 发现低能下存在多种最大纠缠Bell态及其幺正等价形式，分布于不同的运动学区域；质子和中子的纠缠模式存在明显差异，特别是中子的电磁极化率对纠缠模式乃至纠缠的存在性产生决定性影响。

Conclusion: 自旋纠缠为研究核子电磁极化率等性质提供了新颖而灵敏的量子信息工具，有望开启以纠缠为探针的核子结构研究新途径。

Abstract: We study spin-spin entanglement in the final state photon-nucleon system in Compton scattering, both at low energy below the pion threshold and at high energy in perturbative QCD to next-to-leading order. We first establish a no-go theorem showing that, for any spin-$\frac{1}{2}$ target, entanglement cannot be generated in unpolarized Compton scattering if the scattering amplitudes are real. We then consider polarized Compton scattering off the electron, the proton and the neutron. At low energy, we uncover a rich variety of maximally entangled Bell states and their unitary equivalents realized across different regions of the kinematic plane. Interestingly, the proton and neutron targets exhibit distinct patterns of entanglement. In the neutron case, the electric and magnetic polarizabilities dramatically influence the pattern and even the existence of entanglement. This suggests that entanglement can serve as a novel tool for investigating the detailed electromagnetic properties of the nucleons.

</details>


### [103] [HiggsTools for LHC Run 3 and Beyond](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05401)
*Henning Bahl,Thomas Biekötter,Sven Heinemeyer,Kateryna Radchenko Serdula,Georg Weiglein*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文介绍HiggsTools工具箱（含HiggsPredictions、HiggsBounds、HiggsSignals三个子包）的多项改进，为LHC Run 3和HL-LHC做准备，包括新增13.6/14 TeV质心能量下的截面预测、谐振与非谐振希格斯玻色子对产生截面、多top末态搜索的重铸、非谐振希格斯对产生的耦合依赖极限以及具有质量不确定性的标量处理改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着LHC Run 3和HL-LHC的到来，实验数据将大幅增加，需要对BSM标量唯象学工具箱进行更新和扩展，以应对更高能级和新型搜索通道的需求，确保模型预测与实验限制测试的有效性和准确性。

Method: 对HiggsPredictions增加13.6和14 TeV质心能量截面预测以及谐振/非谐振希格斯玻色子对产生截面；对HiggsBounds实现多top末态搜索的重铸、非谐振希格斯对产生的耦合依赖极限及发现前搜索的改进处理；对HiggsSignals改进具有质量不确定性的标量处理。

Result: HiggsTools各子包均获得针对性改进：补充了更高能级截面数据，扩展了希格斯对产生预测，实现了多top末态搜索的重新解读并突出实验灵敏度影响，引入了耦合依赖的极限，优化了早期搜索的处理以及质量不确定性的标量情形。

Conclusion: HiggsTools框架经过全面升级，已为LHC Run 3和HL-LHC的实验结果做好准备，能够更有效地服务于BSM标量唯象学研究。

Abstract: HiggsTools, including the subpackages HiggsPredictions, HiggsBounds, and HiggsSignals, is a toolbox for Beyond-the-SM (BSM) scalar phenomenology at the LHC. It provides BSM model predictions, tests the model against experimental limits from searches for BSM scalars and derives constraints from the measurements of the properties of the discovered Higgs boson. We present a variety of improvements to the HiggsTools framework, preparing it for the results of LHC Run 3 and the HL-LHC. HiggsPredictions now provides additional cross-section predictions for centre-of-mass energies of 13.6 and 14 TeV. Moreover, it now includes cross-section predictions for resonant and non-resonant Higgs-boson pair production. For HiggsBounds, we describe the recasting of searches using multi-top final states, explain their implementation and highlight the impact of the experimental sensitivity of those results. Furthermore, we discuss the implementation of coupling-dependent limits on non-resonant Higgs boson pair production, as well as the improved handling of searches conducted prior to the Higgs boson discovery. For HiggsSignals we describe several improvements for the case of scalars with mass uncertainties.

</details>


### [104] [Spinor tunnelling amplitudes from all-order Feynman diagram summation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05511)
*Max Fleming,Cedric Simenel*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种非微扰量子场论（QFT）计算方法，用于计算自旋子穿过势垒的隧穿过程。通过位置空间势形式体系，推导出自洽的耦合递归关系与积分方程，既能对任意势进行数值计算，也能对特定势垒获得精确解析结果。对于矩形势垒，QFT计算的振幅复现了相对论量子力学（RQM）结果，为未来计算超越RQM的场论修正奠定了基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统相对论量子力学在描述自旋子隧穿时存在局限性，需要发展超越RQM框架的场论计算方法，以获得更精确的物理描述并能为后续场论修正提供理论基矗

Method: 采用非微扰QFT方法，在位置空间势形式体系中建立耦合递归关系，构成自洽的积分方程。该方法同时支持解析与数值求解：数值框架适用于任意势形，解析方法可精确处理特定势垒（如矩形势垒）。

Result: 对于矩形势垒，QFT推导的隧穿振幅与相对论量子力学结果一致，验证了方法的正确性，并建立了计算超越RQM修正的理论框架。

Conclusion: 该非微扰QFT方法为自旋子隧穿计算提供了统一的解析与数值框架，矩形势垒验证结果确认了方法的可靠性，为探索包含辐射修正、真空极化等超越RQM的场论效应铺平了道路。

Abstract: This work presents a non-perturbative quantum field theoretic computation of spinor tunnelling through a potential barrier. Within position space potential formalism, we identify coupled recursion relations forming a self-consistent integral equation. Our method, enabling both analytical and numerical approaches, transforms into a formalism admitting both numerical framework for arbitrary potentials and analytical exact results for select barriers. For the rectangular barrier, the QFT-derived amplitudes reproduce the relativistic quantum mechanical (RQM) results, thus establishing a foundation for future calculation of beyond-RQM field-theoretic corrections.

</details>


### [105] [Coherent and Stochastic Axion Dark Matter from Thermal Relaxation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05538)
*Shahid Hussain Gurmani,Arzu Cilli,Phongpichit Channuie,Ahmadjon Abdujabbarov,Farruh Atamurotov,Ertan Güdekli*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了轴向子（axion）在热等离子体中向热最小態驱动时的衰减行为，推导了场均值衰减与随机动量模产生的耦合关系。一个动量相关的光学深度将每个模划分为相干功率与玻色子占据，统一了标准错位机制、部分弛豫与随机产生。有限时空响应抑制高动量产生，使随机遗迹的均动量和自由流长度小于同温同质量下的平衡分布。弱耦合可实现暗物质丰度、降低相干等曲率扰动，并将产生关联限制在无碰撞传播可消除的尺度上。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解轴向子暗物质在热环境中的完整动力学行为，特别是热弛豫如何决定其组成、动量分布和输运结构，克服以往仅关注相干凝聚体演化的局限。

Method: 在膨胀等离子体中推导场均值衰减与随机动量模产生的耦合方程，引入动量相关的光学深度建立统一动力学关系，分析有限时空响应效应。

Result: 建立了统一关系式，证明有限响应抑制高动量产生，使随机遗迹动量更低、自由流长度更短；弱耦合可实现暗物质丰度并降低相干等曲率扰动。

Conclusion: 热弛豫是决定轴向子暗物质组成、动量分布与输运结构的关键因素，随机产生机制在暗物质遗迹中具有重要物理地位。

Abstract: An axion driven toward a thermal minimum need not remain a coherent condensate. We derive the coupled attenuation of the field mean and production of stochastic momentum modes in an expanding plasma. A single momentum dependent optical depth partitions every mode between coherent power and Bose occupation, placing standard misalignment, partial relaxation, and stochastic production in one dynamical relation. Finite spatial and temporal response suppresses high momentum production, so the stochastic relic has lower mean momentum and a shorter free streaming length than an equilibrium population at fixed mass and bath temperature. A weakly coupled realization produces the dark matter abundance, reduces inherited coherent isocurvature, and confines production correlations to scales removed by collisionless propagation. Thermal relaxation therefore determines the composition, momentum distribution, and transported structure of axion dark matter.

</details>


### [106] [Revisiting the $Λ_c^+ \to nπ^+η$ decay in light of the BESIII measurement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05629)
*Meng-Yuan Li,Jing Tang,Wen-Tao Lyu,Shi-Chen Xue,En Wang*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 基于最新BESIII实验数据，对Λ_c^+→nπ^+η衰变进行系统性理论研究。考虑了动态生成的N(1535)态、a_0(980)共振以及中间态N(1440)和a_2(1320)的贡献。结果表明a_0(980)对该过程贡献显著，而核子共振态在描述π^+η不变质量谱中起关键作用；受限于实验统计量和33 MeV的较粗bin大小，无法可靠提取a_0(980)的确切贡献比例。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: BESIII的最新实验测量为Λ_c^+→nπ^+η衰变提供了新的数据，该衰变涉及强子相互作用中的共振态动力学，特别是a_0(980)和核子激发态的贡献，对这些贡献的系统研究有助于理解强子内部的非微扰QCD动力学。

Method: 采用手征幺正方法(chiral unitary approach)处理S波赝标介子-八重态重子相互作用动态生成N(1535)态，以及S波赝标介子-赝标介子相互作用产生a_0(980)共振；同时纳入N(1440)和a_2(1320)作为中间态贡献，对Λ_c^+→nπ^+η衰变的π^+η不变质量谱进行系统性拟合分析。

Result: a_0(980)对该衰变过程提供显著贡献；引入a_2(1320)几乎不改善拟合质量；核子共振态在描述低能和高能区间的π^+η不变质量谱中均起关键作用。由于实验统计不足且bin大小为33 MeV较粗，无法可靠提取a_0(980)的精确贡献比例。

Conclusion: 该理论分析确认了a_0(980)在Λ_c^+→nπ^+η衰变中的重要贡献以及核子激发态对谱形的关键调控作用。未来更高精度和更高统计量的实验测量有助于揭示a_0(980)的内在本质，并定量评估不同激发核子态在此衰变中的角色。

Abstract: Motivated by the latest BESIII measurements on $Λ_c^+\to nπ^+η$, we perform a systematic theoretical study of this decay. We take into account contributions from the $N(1535)$ state dynamically generated by $S$-wave pseudoscalar meson-octet baryon interactions, the $a_0(980)$ resonance originating from the $S$-wave pseudoscalar meson-pseudoscalar meson interactions, together with the intermediate states $N(1440)$ and $a_2(1320)$. Our results indicate that $a_0(980)$ provides a significant contribution to this process. The inclusion of $a_2(1320)$ hardly improves the fitting quality, while the nucleon resonances play a crucial role in describing the experimental behavior of the $π^+η$ invariant mass spectrum in both low and high energy regions. Restricted by insufficient experimental statistics and a coarse bin size of 33 MeV, the precise contribution fraction of $a_0(980)$ cannot be reliably extracted. We propose future higher-precision and higher-statistics experimental measurements of $Λ_c^+\to nπ^+η$, which can help reveal the intrinsic nature of $a_0(980)$ and quantify the roles of different excited nucleon states in this decay.

</details>


### [107] [Net electron spin rotation in a plane-wave pulse: Holonomy set by the anomalous magnetic moment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05698)
*N. S. Akintsov,A. P. Nevecheria,S. N. Andreev,Qing-Hua Qin*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文计算了相对论电子穿过有限持续时间平面波激光脉冲后存留的自旋旋转。在精确g=2演化的相互作用绘景中，Thomas-Bargmann-Michel-Telegdi方程退化为极化平面上的平移，净旋转即为该联络的holonomy，角度为-½a_e²A（绕传播方向），其中A为横向矢量势所围闭合曲线有向面积的两倍，与光的螺旋度直接相关。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解相对论电子在激光脉冲中传播后的自旋旋转，特别是量化异常磁矩（g-2）与激光脉冲螺旋度之间的耦合效应，以及区分不同情况下（如电子Volkov轨道与中性磁偶极子）的旋转行为差异。

Method: 基于精确g=2的相互作用绘景构建TBMT方程，将其化为具有常系数联络的平行输运问题；利用holonomy（面积律）得到净旋转角度；通过数值模拟（180个脉冲构型验证g=2处的抵消，89个验证面积律）并与两种闭式解（Ternov-Bagrov-Klimenko和Bagrov-Gitman）对比；分析聚焦效应在二阶1/kw0下的修正。

Result: 净旋转角为-½a_e²A，其中a_e=(g-2)/2为异常磁矩，A为矢量势闭合曲线有向面积的两倍。该面积即脉冲每单位面积携带的自旋角动量，因此旋转度量了光的螺旋度。面积律是两种闭式解所界定的二阶普适结果。对于Volkov轨道上的带电子，g/2耦合完全抵消，抑制因子为1.3×10⁻⁶，留下无g无关部分的通道。有限聚焦在二阶1/kw0下恢复该部分，且在γ=10时除非w₀≳16λ、γ=1时除非w₀≳270λ，否则超过异常信号。

Conclusion: 自旋旋转与光的螺旋度之间存在精确的几何关联（面积律），该结果为二阶一般定理，被历史闭式解所界定。带电粒子的Volkov轨道因g/2耦合完全抵消而显著抑制旋转，但聚焦效应可恢复此通道。该工作建立了激光脉冲自旋角动量与电子自旋旋转之间的直接联系。

Abstract: We compute the spin rotation that survives after a relativistic electron has crossed a plane-wave laser pulse of finite duration. In the interaction picture built on the exact $g=2$ evolution, the Thomas-Bargmann-Michel-Telegdi equation becomes parallel transport by a connection with constant coefficients on the polarization plane, and the pulse enters only through the closed curve that the transverse vector potential traces there. The net rotation is the holonomy of that connection: an angle $-\frac{1}{2}a_e^2\mathcal{A}$ about the propagation direction, with $a_e=(g-2)/2$ the anomaly and $\mathcal{A}$ twice the signed area enclosed by the curve. That area is the spin angular momentum the pulse carries per unit area, so the rotation measures the helicity of the light. Reduction of the residual dynamics to a rotation coupled through the anomaly alone is an exact result of the 1960s [Ternov, Bagrov, and Klimenko, Sov. Phys. J. 11, 29 (1968); Bagrov and Gitman, The Dirac Equation and its Solutions (De Gruyter, Berlin, 2014), Sec. 5.3], which yields two closed-form cases; the area law is the general second order that those two cases bound. The same area governs the orientation memory of a neutral magnetic dipole [Oblak and Seraj, Phys. Rev. D 109, 044037 (2024)] with a coupling of order unity. For a charged electron on a Volkov orbit the coupling $g/2$ cancels identically, which suppresses the rotation by $1.3\times10^{-6}$ and leaves a channel with no $g$-independent part. We verify the cancellation at $g=2$ over 180 pulse configurations and the area law over 89 more. Finite focusing restores that part at second order in $1/kw_0$, and it exceeds the anomalous signal unless $w_0\gtrsim16λ$ at $γ=10$, or $270λ$ at $γ=1$.

</details>


### [108] [Machine Learning is Good for Physics - and Vice Versa](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05812)
*Michael Krämer,Tilman Plehn*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文探讨了科学AI对基础物理学研究的快速变革及其挑战，分析了AI与基础物理紧密互动的机遇与风险，强调需在统计验证和泛化理论描述两个关键方面保持对各领域科学方法论的清醒认识，以实现从海量数据中发现新物理的目标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 科学AI正在深刻改变基础物理学研究，并挑战其方法论中定义性的方面。作者希望探讨这一变革带来的机遇和危险，特别是在统计验证和泛化理论描述两个维度上，阐明AI与基础物理如何实现有益互动并从中发现新物理。

Method: 本文通过理论分析和讨论的方式，而非具体的实验或数值方法，聚焦于AI与基础物理交互中的两个核心方法论方面：统计验证（如何在海量数据中严格验证显著性）和泛化理论描述（如何提取超越数据本身的物理理论），以此审视AI带来的变革。

Result: 作者发现AI与基础物理之间的紧密相互作用能带来令人兴奋的益处，但前提是必须对各自领域的科学方法论保持清醒认识，特别是要警惕AI方法可能带来的统计验证问题和理论泛化能力方面的挑战。

Conclusion: 科学AI对基础物理的变革既包含重大机遇也存在需要警惕的危险。实现有益互动的关键在于保持对统计验证和泛化理论描述等基础物理方法论的关注，这样才能在利用AI处理海量数据的同时确保发现的新物理具有可靠的科学基础。

Abstract: Scientific AI is rapidly transforming fundamental physics research and challenging defining aspects of the fundamental physics methodology. We discuss opportunities and dangers of this transformation and find exciting benefits from a close interaction between AI and fundamental physics, provided that we remain aware of the scientific methodologies of the respective fields. For fundamental physics, we discuss two such aspects: statistical validation and a generalizing theory description, both with the goal of discovering new physics in vast datasets.

</details>


### [109] [Descoped and Upscoped FCC-ee Running Scenarios in the SMEFT](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05874)
*Eugenia Celada,Elie Hammou,Jaco ter Hoeve,Marion O. A. Thomas*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文在SMEFT框架下评估了不同降级和升级版FCC-ee方案的物理灵敏度，系统分析了移除顶夸克运行、降低束流功率、减少相互作用点等降级方案以及两种升级方案对物理潜力的影响，指出顶夸克运行对FCC-ee物理潜力至关重要。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: FCC-ee是未来高能物理的重要规划，但面临成本和资金的不确定性，需要系统评估不同运行方案（降级和升级）对新物理灵敏度的影响，以便在资源受限的情况下做出最优决策。

Method: 以基准FCC-ee计划为起点，在SMEFT框架下对多个降级场景（依次移除顶夸克运行、将束流功率从50 MW降至30 MW、移除两个对撞点）进行灵敏度投影，并传播顶夸克质量精度损失带来的参数不确定度增长；同时考虑分阶段恢复顶夸克运行以及两种升级场景（均匀增加亮度或专门增强顶夸克运行亮度），并与(HL)-LHC的测量进行互补性比较。

Result: 各降级和升级场景对FCC-ee的新物理灵敏度有显著不同影响，其中顶夸克运行的移除对物理潜力的损失最为显著；升级方案中针对顶夸克运行的亮度增强效果优于均匀增强方案，且FCC-ee与(HL)-LHC之间表现出良好的测量互补性。

Conclusion: 顶夸克运行承载了FCC-ee大部分物理潜力，其在SMEFT灵敏度中不可替代；在资源有限时，应优先保障顶夸克运行，且针对顶夸克运行的定向升级比均匀升级更具性价比。

Abstract: We present projections for the sensitivity to new physics of descoped and upscoped FCC-ee scenarios in the framework of the Standard Model Effective Field Theory (SMEFT). Starting from the baseline FCC-ee programme, we analyse the subsequent impact of, first, removing the top-quark run, then reducing the beam power from 50 MW to 30 MW, and finally removing two interaction points (IPs), in all cases propagating the resulting loss in top-quark mass precision through increased parametric uncertainties. We also analyse the possibility of a staged top-quark run, where the top-quark run is partially restored in case funding becomes available only at a later stage. Motivated by the trade-off between beam power and operational costs, we also consider two upscoped scenarios, either through a uniform increase in luminosity across all energy runs, or through a luminosity enhancement targeting exclusively the top-quark run. We explore the sensitivity and limitations of each scenario and highlight in particular the complementarity between the FCC-ee projections and the (HL)-LHC measurements. By comparing the physics impact of the different running scenarios with the FCC-ee baseline, we show explicitly the importance of the top-quark run, indicating that a substantial fraction of the FCC-ee physics potential depends on it.

</details>


### [110] [QCD analytic coupling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05894)
*I. A. Zemlyakov,I. L. Chuev,A. V. Kotikov*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文简要概述了QCD解析耦合方法（主要基于文献[1]的结果），并将其应用于π介子-光子跃迁形状因子的计算。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: QCD解析耦合是量子色动力学中处理红外区域非微扰效应的重要方法，本文旨在综述该方法并将其应用于π介子-光子跃迁形状因子这一具体物理过程。

Method: 基于文献[1]中发展的QCD解析耦合方法，对耦合常数的解析性质进行处理，随后将该方法应用于π介子-光子跃迁形状因子的计算。

Result: 综述了QCD解析耦合的关键结果，并展示了该方法在π介子-光子跃迁形状因子计算中的具体应用。

Conclusion: QCD解析耦合方法可以有效地应用于强子物理过程（如π介子-光子跃迁形状因子）的分析计算。

Abstract: A brief overview of QCD analytic coupling is presented, mainly following the results obtained in [1]. An application to the pion-photon transition form factor is demonstrated.

</details>


### [111] [Impact of dimension-8 SMEFT operators on baryogenesis via sphaleron decoupling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05950)
*Kiyoto Ogawa,Masanori Tanaka*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了标准模型有效场论（SMEFT）框架下，通过电弱sphaleron跃迁的CP不对称解耦（即sphalerogenesis）机制能否解释观测到的宇宙重子不对称性。作者引入了七个CP破坏的维度-8算子，发现其中五个能单独解释观测到的重子不对称性，同时满足对撞机或电子电偶极矩测量的实验约束。此外，在存在CP破坏维度-6算子的情况下，当维度-8算子由单圈产生时，其贡献可与维度-6贡献相当，表明在sphalerogenesis中圈阶计数可能与标准质量维度计数同样重要。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 验证标准模型有效场论框架内，一种名为sphalerogenesis的重子生成机制是否能够解释观测到的宇宙重子不对称性，并探究高阶维度算子在该机制中的实际作用与重要性。

Method: 在SMEFT框架下引入七个由希格斯二重态和SU(2)_L规范场构建的CP破坏维度-8算子，系统分析它们各自及与维度-6算子相互作用时对重子不对称性的贡献，并结合对撞机数据和电子电偶极矩测量施加实验约束。

Result: 五个维度-8算子可以单独解释观测到的重子不对称性且满足现有实验约束；在存在维度-6算子时，由单圈产生的维度-8算子贡献可与维度-6贡献相当。

Conclusion: sphalerogenesis是解释宇宙重子不对称性的有效机制，且高阶算子的圈阶效应在此机制中具有重要地位，说明仅按标准质量维度进行算子计数可能低估高阶算子的贡献。

Abstract: We investigate whether a baryogenesis mechanism known as sphalerogenesis can account for the observed baryon asymmetry of the Universe within the Standard Model effective field theory. In this scenario, the baryon asymmetry is generated through the $CP$-asymmetric decoupling of electroweak (EW) sphaleron-like transitions. We introduce seven $CP$-violating dimension-8 operators constructed from the Higgs doublet and the $SU(2)_L$ gauge fields and show that five of them can individually account for the observed baryon asymmetry with satisfying experimental constraints from colliders or electron electric dipole moment measurements. We further study their impact in the presence of a $CP$-violating dimension-6 operator. We find that the dimension-8 contributions can be comparable to the dimension-6 contribution when the dimension-8 operators are generated at one loop, demonstrating that loop-order counting can be as important as canonical mass-dimension counting in sphalerogenesis.

</details>


### [112] [Infrared singularities and the collinear limits of multi-leg scattering amplitudes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06157)
*Sebastian Jaskiewicz*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了无质量散射振幅在多粒子共线极限下的因子化结构，证明到四圈阶，由两粒子共线极限的严格因子化所强加的质量less软反常维度要求，足以保证任意多粒子共线极限下的因子化成立。对于包含有质量彩色粒子的振幅，通过考虑三个无质量粒子的共线极限，推导出软反常维度的新约束。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 散射振幅在软极限和共线极限等特殊运动学极限下具有因子化结构，但多粒子共线极限在高微扰阶下因子化实现的具体机制尚不清楚，特别是对于包含有质量粒子的一般情况。

Method: 通过分析n粒子振幅中m个粒子变得共线时的严格共线因子化要求，在四圈阶验证两粒子共线极限的约束是否充分保证多粒子共线极限的因子化；并对包含有质量彩色粒子的情况，从三个无质量粒子的共线极限导出软反常维度的新约束条件。

Result: 证明了到四圈阶，两粒子共线极限严格因子化对无质量软反常维度所施加的结构要求，足以保证任意多粒子共线极限下的因子化；同时针对包含有质量彩色粒子的振幅，从三粒子共线极限推导出软反常维度的新约束条件。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了多粒子共线极限与两粒子共线极限在因子化结构上的等价性（截至四圈阶），并扩展了包含有质量粒子情形的软反常维度约束，为理解高微扰阶散射振幅的因子化机制提供了新的见解。

Abstract: Scattering amplitudes admit a factorised structure in special kinematic limits, such as the soft and collinear limits. In this work, we investigate the multi-particle collinear limits of massless amplitudes at high perturbative orders, focusing on the exploration of the mechanisms via which strict collinear factorisation of $n$-particle scattering amplitudes is realised when $m$ particles become collinear. We show through four loops that the requirements on the structure of the massless soft anomalous dimension that are imposed by strict collinear factorisation in all two-particle collinear limits are enough to guarantee factorisation also in any multi-particle collinear limit. Demanding that strict collinear factorisation of massless partons is satisfied also for amplitudes that contain a massive coloured particle, we derive new constraints on the soft anomalous dimension by considering the collinear limit of three massless particles.

</details>

### [113] [Tensor Nilpotence and the Size of the Bousfield Lattice](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06104)
*Phil Pützstück*

Main category: math.AT

TL;DR: 该论文对每个素数p和整数n≥1构造了p-局部谱X_n，使得其n次张量积不为零但(n+1)次张量积为零，从而反驳了Hovey-Palmieri的retract猜想；同时确定了Bousfield格基数为2^{2^{ℵ₀}}，回答了Dwyer-Palmieri的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Hovey-Palmieri提出的retract猜想是稳定同伦论中关于p-局部谱张量幂性质的重要猜想。该猜想若成立，将对Bousfield格的结构产生强约束。作者通过构造反例来检验这一猜想的正确性，并进一步探索Bousfield格的具体基数，回答Dwyer-Palmieri的开放问题。

Method: 作者为每个素数p和正整数n构造了一个具体的p-局部谱X_n。该构造的核心性质是其张量幂恰好在第n次不为零、第(n+1)次变为零，即X_n^⊗n ≇ 0但X_n^⊗(n+1) ≃ 0。基于这一构造，作者进一步利用Bousfield格理论分析并确定了Bousfield格的基数。

Result: 成功地构造了满足上述张量幂消失性质的谱族{X_n}，从而给出了Hovey-Palmieri retract猜想的具体反例；并由此确定了Bousfield格的基数为2^{2^{ℵ₀}}，解决了Dwyer-Palmieri的基数问题。

Conclusion: 该工作推翻了Hovey-Palmieri的retract猜想，表明p-局部谱的张量幂行为可以具有更丰富的微妙结构；同时精确计算了Bousfield格的基数，深化了人们对Bousfield格整体结构的理解。

Abstract: For each prime $p$ and integer $n \geq 1$, we construct a $p$-local spectrum $X_n$ with $X_n^{\otimes n} \not\simeq 0$ but $X_n^{\otimes (n+1)} \simeq 0$. This refutes the retract conjecture of Hovey-Palmieri. Our construction also allows us to determine the cardinality of the Bousfield lattice to be $2^{2^{\aleph_0}}$, answering a question of Dwyer-Palmieri.

</details>


### [114] [Whitehead's theorem for minimal finite models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06176)
*David Mosquera-Lois*

Main category: math.AT

TL;DR: 本文研究了Whitehead定理在极小有限模型中的有效性，证明了该定理在此背景下不成立（回答了Barmak提出的问题），并给出了一个正面的Whitehead型结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Whitehead定理是同伦论中的基本定理，但它在极小有限模型这一离散拓扑背景下是否仍然成立是一个自然且重要的问题。Barmak曾提出此问题，作者旨在回答该问题并建立相关分类定理。

Method: (1) 对每个n≥2，构造S^n∨S^{n-1}∨S^{n-1}的两个(2n+4)点的极小有限模型之间的弱同伦等价，证明它们不同伦等价；(2) 证明一个近极值分类定理：若连通有限空间至多有2n+3个点且其第n同调群在某个域上非零，则其序复形同伦等价于S^n或S^n∨S^k（1≤k≤n）；(3) 在自然的上同调刚性假设下证明每个弱同伦等价都是同伦等价。

Result: Whitehead定理在极小有限模型中不成立——构造了反例；同时建立了近极值分类定理，并在上同调刚性条件下获得了正面的Whitehead型结果。

Conclusion: 极小有限模型中的Whitehead定理在一般情况下失效，但在某些上同调刚性条件下仍然成立，这揭示了有限离散空间与经典拓扑空间之间同伦性质的微妙差异。

Abstract: We investigate the extent to which Whitehead's theorem remains valid for minimal finite models. We show that it fails in this setting, answering negatively a question posed by Barmak. More precisely, for every $n\geq 2$, we construct a weak homotopy equivalence between two $(2n+4)$-point minimal finite models of $S^n\vee S^{n-1}\vee S^{n-1}$ which are not homotopy equivalent. The minimality of these examples follows from a near-extremal classification theorem: if a connected finite space has at most $2n+3$ points and nonzero $n$th homology over a field, then its order complex is homotopy equivalent either to $S^n$ or to $S^n\vee S^k$ for some $1\leq k\leq n$. Finally, we prove a positive Whitehead-type result: under a natural cohomological rigidity hypothesis, every weak homotopy equivalence between minimal finite models is a homotopy equivalence.

</details>


### [115] [The Intersection Euler Characteristic Profile: Euler Calculus and Stability for Topological Interaction of Ball Unions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06180)
*Kazuhiro Kawamura,Sushovan Majhi,Atish Mitra*

Main category: math.AT

TL;DR: 本文提出交集欧拉特征轮廓（Intersection ECP），一种多参数不变式，用于刻画k个彩色点云的拓扑交互。该不变量基于欧拉演算的构造性函数框架，将几何交集与代数乘积对应起来（交集定理），具有刚性不变性、尺度等变性、L1稳定性等性质，并可通过单个Alpha复形扫描在O(n^⌈d/2⌉ log n)时间内计算。同时提出相对同调细化用于处理欧拉特征抵消的情形，并证明了在稠密采样下的一致性估计性质。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为多个点云集合之间的拓扑交互提供系统化的多参数不变量描述。现有工作多关注单个点云或多点云的单参数描述（如持久同调），缺乏对k个点云在不同尺度下交互行为（交集）的结构化、理论完备的刻画框架。

Method: 用欧拉演算/构造性函数的视角定义交集欧拉特征轮廓：χ(U_i X_i; t_i)的欧拉特征值函数。证明交集定理（欧拉积分与乘积的交换），利用构造性微积分将几何交集转化为代数乘积。通过Alpha复形扫描实现高效计算，并引入相对同调细化解决欧拉特征抵消问题。

Result: 该不变量具有刚性运动不变性、尺度等变性和L1稳定性；在多云交汇描述子谱系中具有典范地位。计算复杂度为O(n^⌈d/2⌉ log n)（偶维最优）。相对同调细化在双参数交叠距离下稳定。对稠密样本具有一致性：紧集情形下恢复逆极限中的同调，正到达条件下具有显式样本复杂度的持久恢复。

Conclusion: 交集欧拉特征轮廓为多尺度多点云拓扑交互提供了理论完备、计算高效且具有稳定性和一致性的系统性框架，兼具理论与算法价值。

Abstract: The Intersection Euler Characteristic Profile (Intersection ECP) of $k$ colored point clouds $X_1, \ldots, X_k \subset \mathbb{R}^d$ is the Euler characteristic $χ(\bigcap_{i=1}^k \mathcal{U}(X_i; t_i))$ of the overlap of their ball unions---an integer-valued, multiparameter invariant of their topological interaction across scales. Its organizing framework is the Euler calculus on constructible functions: the profile is equally the Euler integral $\int \prod_{i=1}^k \mathbf{1}_{\mathcal{U}(X_i; t_i)} \, dχ$ of the product of the $k$ data-dependent offsets, and this identity---our Intersection Theorem---is a commuting square interchanging geometric intersection and algebraic product. The invariant is rigid-motion invariant, scale-equivariant, and $L^1$-stable, and it is canonical: among pointwise-Euler interaction profiles it is the one forced by separation and normalization, the top floor of a spectrum of descriptors graded by how many clouds meet. For $n$ points a single sorted Alpha-complex sweep computes it in $O(n^{\lceil d/2 \rceil} \log n)$ time with no persistence reduction, worst-case optimal in even dimensions. Where the Euler characteristic cancels, a relative-homology refinement resolves the finer interaction and is stable in the two-parameter interleaving distance. Finally, for increasingly dense samples the profile and its refinement are consistent, recovering the (relative) homology and Euler characteristic of the underlying shapes---in the inverse limit for compact sets, and, under positive reach, persistently and with explicit sample complexity.

</details>


### [116] [A stable framework for proper and exterior homotopy and cohomology theories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06186)
*Antonio Ceres,José M. García-Calcines,Aniceto Murillo*

Main category: math.AT

TL;DR: 本文为外部同伦理论建立了一个稳定的框架，并将其应用于正常同伦理论。作者从已知的外部空间单纯模型结构出发，构造了射线上的收缩外部空间的正常胞腔模型范畴，并建立了外部谱的稳定同伦理论。该框架提供了稳定同伦理论中若干经典构造的外部类比，并阐明了正常同伦理论。作为应用，作者建立了约化外部上同调理论的Brown可表示定理，从而为正常上同调理论（包括与经典广义上同调理论相关的紧支上同调理论）提供了可表示性结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 正常同伦理论中令人满意的稳定理论的发展一直受到缺乏适当范畴性质的阻碍。本文旨在通过建立一个稳定的外部同伦理论框架来弥补这一不足，从而为正常同伦理论提供新的视角和有力的工具。

Method: 从已知的外部空间单纯模型结构出发，过渡到射线上的收缩外部空间的正常胞腔模型范畴，建立外部谱的稳定同伦理论，为稳定同伦理论中的经典构造提供外部类比。

Result: 建立了约化外部上同调理论的Brown可表示定理，获得了正常上同调理论（包括与经典广义上同调理论相关的紧支上同调理论）的可表示性结果。

Conclusion: 本文成功地将稳定同伦理论的核心思想推广到外部同伦理论，为正常同伦理论提供了稳定的理论框架，并通过Brown可表示定理建立了外部与正常上同调理论的可表示性基础。

Abstract: We develop a stable framework for exterior homotopy theory and apply it to proper homotopy theory. Starting from a known simplicial model structure on the category of exterior spaces we then pass to the proper, cellular model category of retractive exterior spaces over the ray, and establish the associated stable homotopy theory of exterior spectra.
  The resulting framework provides exterior analogues of several classical constructions in stable homotopy theory and sheds new light on proper homotopy theory, where the development of a satisfactory stable theory has been hindered by the lack of suitable categorical properties. As an application, we establish a Brown representability theorem for reduced exterior cohomology theories. This yields representability results for proper cohomology theories arising from the exterior setting, including cohomology theories with compact supports associated with classical generalized cohomology theories.

</details>


<div id='hep-th'></div>

# hep-th [[Back]](#toc)

### [117] [Tree-Level Factorization Obstruction in Monopole Production](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05272)
*Hsing-Yi Lai,John Terning*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文证明，仅通过最小单光子耦合无法构造从带电粒子对产生磁单极-反磁单极对的树级振幅。电与磁三点顶点拼接产生的光子极点贡献会导致磁初态与电末态具有相反的离散对称性本征值，而局域四点相互作用无法修复这一不匹配。对于费米子，Pauli形状因子可提供自旋单态三点振幅，从而满足对称性选择规则。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究在电磁对偶框架下，磁单极对产生过程的树级振幅能否仅由最小耦合构建，特别是检验离散对称性（如C、P、T）对振幅结构的约束。

Method: 通过拼接电和磁的三点顶点来构造光子极点贡献，分析其离散对称性本征值，并考察局域四点相互作用的局限性；对于费米子情况，引入独立的Pauli形状因子来绕过对称性障碍。

Result: 发现最小单光子耦合无法构造自洽的单极对产生振幅，因为电初态与磁末态具有相反的离散对称性本征值；局域四点相互作用无法修复极点贡献；而费米子的Pauli形状因子（自旋单态三点振幅）可以满足对称性选择规则。

Conclusion: 要正确构造磁单极对产生的树级振幅，需要超越最小耦合，引入非最小耦合项（如Pauli形状因子），这暗示了磁单极物理中需要更丰富的相互作用结构。

Abstract: We show that the tree-level amplitude for producing a monopole--antimonopole pair from an electrically charged pair cannot be constructed from the minimal one-photon couplings. Gluing the electric and magnetic three-point vertices gives a nonzero photon-pole contribution, but the resulting magnetic final state has the opposite discrete-symmetry eigenvalue from the electric initial state. Local four-point interactions cannot change the pole contribution fixed by the three-point couplings, so they cannot repair this mismatch. For fermions, the obstruction is specific to the minimal three-point coupling: an independent Pauli form factor supplies a spin-singlet three-point amplitude and allows the symmetry selection rule to be satisfied.

</details>


### [118] [Moduli Bounds from Spin-2 Sum Rules](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05273)
*Francesco Bertucci,James Bonifacio,Kurt Hinterbichler*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文利用共形bootstrap方法证明了引力Kaluza-Klein理论中轻标量场质量的上界：最轻KK引力子（质量m1）必然耦合到某个标量，满足(m_sc/m1)^2 ≤ 4/3；且每个更重的KK引力子（质量mn）必然耦合到某个标量，满足(m_sc/mn)^2 < 36/25。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Mirbabayi和Villadoro此前通过数值证据提出了引力KK理论中一个普适上界猜想，即最轻KK引力子必须耦合到足够轻的标量场。该猜想对于理解KK理论的低能有效场论结构具有重要意义，但缺乏严格的解析证明。

Method: 本文采用共形bootstrap方法，将其应用于有质量自旋-2散射振幅的求和规则中，从而从第一性原理出发严格推导该上界。

Result: 1) 证明了最轻KK引力子质量m1所耦合标量的质量满足(m_sc/m1)^2 ≤ 4/3；2) 推广到每个更重的KK引力子，证明其耦合标量质量满足(m_sc/mn)^2 < 36/25。

Conclusion: 本文为该普适上界提供了严格的理论证明，并进一步推广至所有更重KK引力子，深化了对引力KK理论中质量谱结构的理解。

Abstract: Mirbabayi and Villadoro have provided strong numerical evidence for a universal upper bound on the mass of the lightest scalar field in gravitational Kaluza--Klein (KK) theories. This bound states that the lightest KK graviton, with mass $m_{1}$, must couple to at least one scalar with mass $m_{\rm sc}$ such that $(m_{\rm sc} /m_{1})^2 \leq 4/3$. We give a proof of this bound using the approach of the conformal bootstrap applied to sum rules for massive spin-2 scattering amplitudes. We additionally show that each heavier KK graviton, with mass $m_n$, must couple to at least one scalar with mass $m_{\rm sc}$ such that $(m_{\rm sc} /m_{n})^2 < 36/25$.

</details>


### [119] [Adiabatic Deformations of Black Hole Moduli: I. Canonical Slices and Fredholm Solvability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05275)
*Karim Benakli,Anna Chrysostomou*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究了静态黑洞在缓慢演化的渐近模量下的绝热响应问题，将其分解为几何问题和泛函分析问题，并证明二者均可通过显式标量判据刻画。通过区分精确动力学视界与瞬时代表视界，利用局部切片移除切向歧义，引入了约化算子的有限秩扰动，在横截性和Fredholm假设下，将存在唯一性问题归结为单个标量匹配条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究静态黑洞对外部渐近模量缓慢演化的绝热响应，此前该问题缺乏统一的几何与泛函分析框架。作者希望将这一复杂问题分解为可处理的几何层面（确定黑洞实际跟随的切向方向）和泛函分析层面（判断形变是否具有唯一解），并给出显式可计算的判据。

Method: 首先，对静态解的平滑族进行微分定义切空间上的内蕴余向量，其在由渐近模量与静态视界质量参数化时，其核识别出与缓慢变化外部模量所选方向一致的规范切向。其次，将静态族提升为缓慢演化的瞬时代表，虽不满足精确场方程，但产生的差异定义一个拉格朗日场，在消去度规扰动后由单一约化径向算子支配。采用表示约定区分精确动力学视界与瞬时代表视界，通过局部切片移除剩余切向歧义，诱导约化算子的有限秩扰动，保持Fredholm指标不变。最后在横截性和Fredholm假设下，将边界值问题归结为核生成元上的单个标量匹配条件。

Result: 证明了绝热响应的几何方向与泛函可解性均可由显式标量判据刻画；规范切向方向与外部模量缓慢变化所选方向一致；有限秩扰动保持Fredholm指标，且在非平凡更新下规范切向零模从核中位移；存在唯一性归结为单一标量匹配条件。整个构造仅依赖平滑非极端静态解流形及约化算子的Fredholm性质。

Conclusion: 本文建立了静态黑洞绝热响应的完整框架，将复杂的几何与泛函分析问题简化为显式标量判据，结果适用于一般平滑非极端静态解流形。该框架为后续专门研究磁GHS族的伴生论文提供了通用基础。

Abstract: The leading adiabatic response of a static black hole to a slowly evolving asymptotic modulus separates into a geometric problem and a functional-analytic one: the former identifies the tangent direction on the manifold of static solutions actually followed by the black hole, and the latter determines whether the resulting deformation admits a unique solution. We show that both can be characterized by explicit scalar criteria. Differentiating a smooth family of static solutions defines an intrinsic covector on its tangent space. When the family is parameterized by the asymptotic modulus and the static horizon-mass parameter, the kernel of this covector identifies a canonical tangent direction that coincides with the one selected by a slowly varying exterior modulus. Promoting the static family to a slowly evolving instantaneous representative does not solve the exact field equations; the resulting discrepancy defines a lag field governed, after elimination of the metric perturbations, by a single reduced radial operator. A representation convention separates the exact dynamical horizon from that of the instantaneous representative. A local slice then removes the remaining tangential ambiguity and induces a finite-rank perturbation of the reduced operator. The Fredholm index is preserved, and, whenever the finite-rank update is nontrivial, the canonical tangent zero mode is displaced from the kernel of the reduced operator. Under the transversality and Fredholm hypotheses, existence and uniqueness of the completed leading-order adiabatic boundary-value problem reduce to a single scalar matching condition on the resulting kernel generator. The construction depends only on a smooth non-extremal static solution manifold and the Fredholm properties of the reduced operator. A companion paper specializes the framework to the magnetic GHS family.

</details>


### [120] [Higher-Spin Correlators in dS](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05278)
*Shounak De,Hayden Lee*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究了dS4中最小高自旋引力理论在动量空间的关联函数，利用全息矢量模型描述。通过三维单圈积分表示n点关联函数，发现四点函数存在由Ptolemy关系控制的几何奇异结构，五点函数的表观Landau奇点被证明是伪奇点，并给出了无伪奇点的表示。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解de Sitter空间中高自旋引力的全息对应物及其动量空间关联函数的几何与组合结构，探索一种基于图论的组合引导方法（combinatorial bootstrap）来构造一般n点的高自旋关联函数。

Method: 利用全息矢量模型将高自旋引力在dS4中的动量空间关联函数表示为三维单圈积分；通过分析Landau奇点、施加Gram约束来消除伪奇点；引入图论构建块来组织五点函数表示；验证共形不变性并研究软极限、坍缩极限与Ptolemy极限。

Result: 四点函数展现出由双动量四边形Ptolemy关系控制的非平凡物理奇点；五点函数中表观的前导Landau奇点在施加Gram约束后被证明是伪奇点，并得到了无伪奇点、基于图论构建块的表示；关联函数满足共形不变性。

Conclusion: 高自旋关联函数的物理奇点和共形不变性可由底层图组合学产生，提示存在一种面向任意点数的组合引导方法，为理解dS空间引力-边界理论对应提供了新视角。

Abstract: We study momentum-space correlators in minimal higher-spin gravity in $\mathrm{dS}_4$ using its holographic vector-model description. Connected $n$-point functions in this model are represented by three-dimensional one-loop integrals and are rational functions of the boundary kinematics. We show that the four-point function displays a nontrivial geometric feature: its physical singularity is governed by a Ptolemy relation for the dual momentum quadrilateral. At five points, the pentagon integral introduces an apparent leading Landau singularity, which we show is spurious and disappears once the Gram constraint is imposed. We derive a representation of the five-point function free of spurious singularities and organized in terms of graph-theoretic building blocks. We then verify the conformal invariance of these correlators and discuss their behavior in the soft, collapsed, and Ptolemy limits. This structure suggests a combinatorial bootstrap for higher-spin correlators at general multiplicity, in which conformal invariance and the physical singularities emerge from the underlying graph combinatorics.

</details>


### [121] [Mass and Force Relations for Extremal E2MD Black Holes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05280)
*Sera Cremonini,Mirjam Cvetič,Christopher N. Pope,Aritra Saha*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究爱因斯坦引力与dilaton及两个Maxwell场耦合的静态极端黑洞。在四维中b=-1/a的特殊参数轨迹上，多黑洞构型的相互作用力为零。通过利用常数dilaton平移对称性、齐次性和极端性条件，推导出一阶常微分方程来确定极端质量与电荷的关系，从而无需显式黑洞几何即可分析非相同极端黑洞之间的引力。研究扩展到任意时空维度，并利用A2、B2、G2 Toda黑洞验证了力符号规律。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解极端黑洞之间的长程力行为对于黑洞物理学和超引力理论具有重要意义。以往精确多黑洞解仅在特定参数下可得，而作者希望发展一种通用方法，无需知道显式黑洞几何就能判断任意两个非相同极端黑洞之间的力是吸引还是排斥，从而揭示参数空间中的力符号结构及其与几何正则性的联系。

Method: 首先在四维中b=-1/a的特殊参数轨迹上利用时间对称初值构造确定多黑洞的质量和相互作用能，发现该轨迹上长程力消失。对于一般a和b，利用dilaton常数平移对称性、齐次性和极端性条件推导出一阶常微分方程，确定极端质量作为两个电荷的函数。该方程使作者能不依赖显式几何就分析非相同极端黑洞间的力。分析扩展到d维时空，得到力消除条件ab=-2(d-2)/(d-1)。最后借助A2、B2和G2 Toda黑洞精确解进行验证。

Result: 在所有解析可控的区域中，b=-1/a轨迹将b>-1/a的吸引行为与b<-1/a的排斥行为分开。在d维中对应力消除条件为ab=-2(d-2)/(d-1)。通过精确Toda黑洞验证了符号模式。G2情形中一个潜在的有问题分支因存在视界外的裸奇点而被排除，暗示了黑洞几何正则性与长程力符号之间的有趣联系。

Conclusion: 作者建立了极端黑洞间长程力的普适符号判据，无需显式解得黑洞几何即可判断力性质。该结论在精确Toda解中得到验证。G2情形中排除的非物理分支揭示了正则性与力的符号之间的深层联系，这可能对黑洞解的分类和极值态的性质有重要启示。

Abstract: We study static extremal black holes in Einstein gravity coupled to a dilaton and two Maxwell fields with independent dilaton couplings $a$ and $b$. When $b=-1/a$ in four dimensions, a time-symmetric initial-value construction allows us to determine the masses and interaction energies of multi-black-hole configurations. On this special locus, the long-range force between any two extremal black holes vanishes. For arbitrary $a$ and $b$, a constant dilaton-shift symmetry, together with homogeneity and the extremality condition, yields a first-order ordinary differential equation that determines the extremal mass as a function of the two electric charges. This equation allows us to analyze the force between non-identical extremal black holes without having to know the explicit black-hole geometry. In all analytically controlled regimes that we examine, the locus $b=-1/a$ separates attractive behavior for $b>-1/a$ from repulsive behavior for $b<-1/a$. We extend the analysis to arbitrary spacetime dimensions, where the corresponding force-cancellation condition is $ab=-2(d-2)/(d-1)$. Finally, we test this sign pattern using the exact $A_2$, $B_2$ and $G_2$ Toda black holes. In the $G_2$ case, a potentially problematic branch is excluded because it contains naked singularities outside the horizon, suggesting an intriguing connection between regularity and the sign of long-range forces.

</details>


### [122] [Exact Lattice Identities and Continuum-Limit Dyson--Schwinger Equations for Yang-Mills Theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05415)
*Arpan Chatterjee,Marco Frasca,Anish Ghoshal,Stefan Groote*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文从SU(N)格点规范理论出发，利用Haar测度在左群平移下的不变性，直接在紧致规范群上用链变量严格推导出连续极限下的Dyson-Schwinger方程。随后在Feynman规范下分析格点恒等式的连续极限形式，推导一阶和二阶关联函数方程，并在简化约化下闭合为标量系统，建立了格点精确恒等式与连续非微扰泛函方程之间的直接桥梁。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的Dyson-Schwinger方程推导在有限格距下直接引入李代数取值的规范势作为积分变量存在模糊性，需要一种在紧致规范群上直接操作的无歧义推导方法。

Method: 利用Haar测度在左群平移下的不变性，直接在链变量U_μ(m)∈SU(N)上推导格点Dyson-Schwinger恒等式，通过左不变李导数表达Wilson作用量的主方程；随后选取Feynman规范分解规范自由度，分析连续极限形式并推导单点和两点关联函数方程，最终在简化约化下得到可解的标量系统。

Result: 获得了精确的格点Dyson-Schwinger方程，在Feynman规范下建立了其连续极限形式，推导出一阶和二阶关联函数方程，并在进一步约化下得到闭合的标量系统。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了格点精确Dyson-Schwinger恒等式与连续非微扰Yang-Mills泛函方程之间的直接联系，为格点正则化与连续极限方程的一致性提供了严格基础。

Abstract: Starting from SU(N) on the lattice, we give a rigorous derivation of the Dyson--Schwinger equations in the continuum limit. We formulate the Dyson--Schwinger identities for the lattice Yang--Mills theory directly in terms of the link variables $U_μ(m)\in SU(N)$, exploiting the invariance of the Haar measure under left group translations. This provides an exact lattice derivation of the corresponding master equation for the Wilson action, expressed through left-invariant Lie derivatives acting on individual links. Because the construction is carried out directly on the compact gauge group, it avoids the ambiguities associated with introducing Lie-algebra valued gauge potentials as primary integration variables at finite lattice spacing. For practical applications, in a second part we then break down the gauge degree of freedom by choosing Feynman gauge. We analyze the continuum-limit form of the resulting lattice identities and derive equations for the one- and two-point connected functions. Under a further simplifying reduction, these equations close to a tractable scalar system. Our results establish a direct bridge between exact lattice identities and the functional equations commonly used in continuum nonperturbative studies of Yang--Mills theory.

</details>


### [123] [$N$-Photon Amplitudes in EFT from Recursion Relations and Effective Vertices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05285)
*Gustavo Bispo,Sylvain Fichet*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个递归框架，用于计算一般电磁有效场论（EFT）中任意树级螺旋度振幅。该方法基于CSW-like递归，构建块为纯局域子振幅（接触振幅），可用Hafnian紧凑表达。并建立了一个有效的接触拉格朗日量系统（提供至N=14光子），揭示了螺旋度守恒振幅的隐藏简洁性，证明了离壳电磁对偶与螺旋度守恒的等价性。作者还展示了如何对螺旋度守恒理论的接触拉格朗日量进行全阶重求和（以Born-Infeld为例），并计算了通用EFT算子的完整6点和8点树级振幅以及BI电磁学的10点树级振幅。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在一般电磁EFT中，由于存在无穷多种有效算符，直接计算螺旋度振幅通常非常复杂。作者旨在建立一种系统化、可递归的方法来计算任意树级振幅，揭示螺旋度振幅中隐藏的简单结构，并连接电磁对偶与螺旋度守恒。

Method: (1) 提出CSW-like递归构造光子振幅，构建块为纯局域的接触子振幅；(2) 对固定外腿数，只需计算有限个接触振幅，可用Hafnian紧凑表达；(3) 通过合适的生成泛函，从一般EFT拉格朗日量系统性地计算有效的接触拉格朗日量（提供至N=14光子）；(4) 对螺旋度守恒理论展示接触拉格朗日量的全阶重求和（以Born-Infeld为例）；(5) 利用这些工具计算通用EFT的6点和8点完整树级振幅及BI电磁学的10点树级振幅。

Result: 成功提供了至N=14光子的接触拉格朗日量；给出了螺旋度守恒理论与离壳电磁对偶等价性的简洁证明；展示了Born-Infeld理论中接触拉格朗日量的全阶重求和；计算了通用EFT的完整6点和8点树级螺旋度振幅，以及BI电磁学的达到10点的树级振幅。

Conclusion: 该递归框架为一般电磁EFT的树级振幅计算提供了系统化方案，接触拉格朗日量揭示了螺旋度守恒的隐藏简单性，并建立了电磁对偶与螺旋度守恒之间的等价关系。该方法可推广至更高阶点振幅及其他EFT场景。

Abstract: We present a recursive framework for computing arbitrary tree-level helicity amplitudes in the general effective field theory (EFT) of electromagnetism. We show that photon amplitudes can be constructed using a CSW-like recursion, whose building blocks consist of purely local subamplitudes. For a fixed number of external legs, only a finite number of these contact amplitudes need to be computed, and these admit compact expressions in terms of Hafnians. We further show that the contact amplitudes are encoded in an effective contact Lagrangian, that can be computed systematically from the general EFT Lagrangian using a suitable generating functional. We provide this contact Lagrangian up to $N=14$ photons. We argue that the contact Lagrangian reveals the hidden simplicity of helicity-conserving amplitudes, providing a simple proof of the equivalence between off-shell electromagnetic duality and helicity conservation. We further show how to resum the contact Lagrangian of a helicity-conserving theory to all $N$, and illustrate the procedure for Born-Infeld (BI) electromagnetism. Building on these methods and results, we compute the complete 6-point and 8-point tree-level helicity amplitudes generated by generic EFT operators, and the tree amplitudes up to 10-point in BI electromagnetism.

</details>


### [124] [Adiabatic Deformations of Black Hole Moduli: II. The Magnetic GHS Family](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05291)
*Karim Benakli,Anna Chrysostomou*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究了无质量Einstein-Maxwell-dilaton理论中磁GHS黑洞对外部缓变模量的绝热响应，通过规范切片与Fredholm框架推导了完整的准静态近区解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解动态黑洞对外部缓变扰动的绝热响应是引力理论中的重要问题。磁GHS黑洞族因其可积性，为实现规范和Fredholm框架提供了理想模型，从而可用于严格证明准静态近区问题的存在唯一性。

Method: 在Eddington-Finkelstein坐标中采用精确面半径，推导随时间变化的球对称方程，围绕缓慢演化的瞬时静态GHS代表解展开。利用磁GHS族的可积性，以闭式形式得到规范切向量、有限秩源剖面、辅助非齐次方程的视界正则解以及切片算子核的全局正则生成子。进一步解析计算Fredholm分母并证明其在带电非极端分支上严格为正。

Result: 证明Fredholm分母在带电非极端分支上严格为正，表明所谓的核模态简并点实为规范奇异点而非真正的退化。显式求解受迫径向方程：标量滞后由普适受迫剖面与切片齐次模之和构成，质量滞后由径向约束代数给出，时滞滞后由单个初等积分得到，并可从滞后修正的解中恢复精确的边际视界。

Conclusion: 该工作完成了无质量重叠模型下每个可容许外部数据的完整领先阶准静态近区响应，其径向依赖性精确，为动态黑洞视界响应理论提供了严格且可解析验证的基准结果。

Abstract: We study the leading adiabatic response of magnetic GHS black holes in massless Einstein-Maxwell-dilaton theory to a prescribed exterior modulus varying slowly in advanced time. In ingoing Eddington-Finkelstein coordinates with the exact areal radius, we derive the time-dependent spherical equations and expand about a slowly evolving instantaneous static GHS representative, keeping its horizon distinct from the exact dynamical marginal horizon. The magnetic GHS family realizes the canonical-slice and Fredholm framework developed in the companion paper. Its integrability yields in closed form the canonical tangent, the finite-rank source profile, a horizon-regular solution of the auxiliary inhomogeneous equation, and a globally regular generator of the sliced-operator kernel. For the leading Robin functional arising in the massless large-radius overlap, we evaluate the Fredholm denominator analytically and prove that it is strictly positive on the charged non-extremal branch. This remains true where the conventional normalization of the kernel generator degenerates, showing that these loci are normalization singularities rather than degeneracies of the kernel mode. Within the controlled large-radius overlap regime, this positivity proves existence and uniqueness of the completed leading-order quasi-static near-zone problem. We then solve the forced radial equation explicitly. The scalar lag is the sum of a universal forced profile and a sliced homogeneous mode whose amplitude is fixed by the exterior matching datum. The mass lag follows algebraically from the radial constraint, and the lapse lag from a single elementary quadrature. The exact marginal horizon is recovered from the lag-corrected solution. The result gives the complete leading quasi-static near-zone response, exact in its radial dependence, for every admissible exterior datum within the specified massless overlap model.

</details>


### [125] [Interacting Galilean and Finite-Energy Carroll Fermions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05324)
*Pulastya Parekh,Aditya Sharma,Mauricio Valenzuela*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文基于相似变换统一推导了有质量Dirac作用量的Galilean与Carrollian极限，通过不同的Dirac共轭选择与质量标度生成多个不同族系的极限作用量，并分类了与boost相容的局域(自)相互作用（含NJL）。新得到的Galilean自由作用量具有局部费米子规范对称性，可消除其局部场内容；而选择的四次相互作用会显式破缺该规范对称性。Carroll作用量中的能量参数可通过含时相位重定义消除，但新的Carrollian推广不能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在非相对论极限（Galilean）和超相对论极限（Carrollian）下，狄拉克费米子的作用量形式尚未被系统地统一推导。作者希望借助相似变换给出一个有质量Dirac作用量的统一处理框架，从而系统地生成和分类这两类极限中的费米子模型，并探索其相互作用结构。

Method: 方法基于一个依赖质量m和光速c的相似变换来取Galilean和Carrollian极限。通过两种Dirac共轭选择与不同的质量标度组合，可生成不同族系的作用量。作者同时分类满足boost相容条件的局域(自)相互作用（包含NJL型），并分析各极限作用量的规范对称性（如费米子规范对称性及Bargmann boost）及其随相互作用加入的破缺或变形情况；对Carroll作用量还研究了通过含时相位重定义消除能量参数的可行性。

Result: 该构造恢复了已知的Galilei和Carroll费米子模型，并各得到一个新的系统。新Galilean自由作用量具有局部费米子规范对称性，可消除局部场内容；选定的四次相互作用在保持Bargmann boost的同时显式破缺该规范对称性，使场内容消除论证失效；NJL极限作用量只是deform了该规范对称性的实现。多个Carroll作用量中的能量参数可被含时相位重定义消除，但新Carrollian推广中的能量无法移除。

Conclusion: 该统一推导框架不仅再现了已知结果，还发现了新的Galilean与Carrollian费米子系统，并揭示了它们各自的规范对称性特征及相互作用对对称性的影响。这一工作为研究非相对论与超相对论极限下费米子场论的结构提供了系统化的工具和新见解。

Abstract: We present a unified derivation of the Galilean and Carrollian limits of the massive Dirac action based on a similarity transformation depending on the mass and the speed of light. The two choices of Dirac conjugation, combined with different mass scalings, generate distinct families of limiting actions. This construction recovers known Galilei and Carroll fermion models and yields new systems, one in each regime. We classify a sufficient set of boost-compatible local (self-)interactions, which includes Nambu--Jona-Lasinio (NJL). The new Galilean free action possesses a local fermionic gauge symmetry that eliminates its local field content. A selected quartic interaction, preserving the Bargmann boost, explicitly breaks the original fermionic gauge symmetry so that the argument that eliminates the field content no longer applies. The NJL limit interaction merely deforms the realization of that gauge symmetry. We show that the energy parameter in several Carroll actions can be eliminated by a time-dependent phase redefinition; conversely, in the new Carrollian extension the energy cannot be removed.

</details>

Result: Schwarzschild解在对应面处恰好饱和标称α'阈值，而所有带标量毛的FJNW分支均高于该阈值。与共同归一化无关，在对应面处任何非零毛分支的曲率诊断量均大于Schwarzschild。在远离对应点时，若局域自身引力足够弱且局域弦耦合也弱，带标量毛的外区域可保持在α'控制之下，为高度激发弦态提供微扰可控的长距离场。

Conclusion: 轴子-伸缩子荷空间可通过SL(2,R)轨道完整刻画；弦-黑洞对应准则表明Schwarzschild处于α'阈值的临界饱和位置，而带标量毛的解在对应面处曲率修正更强，但在特定弱耦合条件下也能提供微扰有效的长距离弦态描述。

Abstract: We study static, spherically symmetric axion--dilaton solutions of the tree-level four-dimensional string effective action. Using the sigma-model structure of the scalar sector, we show that the full family of static, spherically symmetric and asymptotically flat solutions is obtained as the $SL(2,\mathbb R)$ orbit of the pure-dilaton FJNW solution, and we characterize the associated axion--dilaton charge space. We then analyze the perturbative regime of these solutions by comparing the onset of $α'$ curvature corrections with that of string-loop corrections. Finally, we apply the string--black-hole correspondence criterion to the FJNW family by evaluating the local $α'$ curvature diagnostic at the physical size $R_{\rm typ}$ of a highly excited string state. With the normalization fixed on the Schwarzschild branch, Schwarzschild saturates the nominal $α'$ threshold at the correspondence surface, whereas every scalar-haired FJNW branch lies above it. More generally, independently of the common overall normalization within the matching prescription, every nonzero-hair branch has a larger curvature diagnostic at the correspondence surface than Schwarzschild. Away from the correspondence point, scalar-haired exteriors may remain under $α'$ control at sufficiently weak local self-gravity and may provide perturbatively controlled long-distance fields of highly excited string states whenever the local string coupling also remains weak.

Abstract: We study the reflected entropy and the Markov gap for modes of a free bosonic field shared by inertial observers (Alice, Charlie) and a uniformly accelerated one (Bob), for a bipartite Bell state and the tripartite Werner (W) and Greenberger--Horne--Zeilinger (GHZ) states. The bosonic Bogoliubov transformation spans an infinite-dimensional Fock space with an unbounded squeezing parameter unlike the fermionic case. By identifying a conserved charge, we block-diagonalize the reduced density matrices into exact two-dimensional sectors, yielding closed or semi-analytic forms for all three states. Although bosonic entanglement is known to vanish asymptotically, the Alice--Bob reflected entropy instead saturates at a nonzero saturation floor, retaining the surviving classical correlation, and converges to the value Alice shares with Bob's causally disconnected partner. Crucially, only the inter-wedge reflected entropy diverges, linearly in the squeezing parameter---the sharp distinction from the fermionic case, where it stays bounded---while the Markov gaps saturate. The construction transfers verbatim to a Schwarzschild black hole, where the saturation values become mass-independent constants.

Abstract: We develop an ODE/IM-based formulation for the thermal partition function and the spectral zeta function of the homogeneous Hermitian and PT-symmetric oscillators. Result: 成功证明了手征反常与共形反常之间存在精确的双拷贝关系。该关系从局域反常结构一致延伸到完整的动量空间三点关联函数，并通过平直空间极限与五维散射振幅建立联系。具体而言，手征反常对应的奇宇称流三点关联函数在平直空间极限下约化为由Chern-Simons相互作用生成的普适振幅，对这些关联函数进行平方运算后精确复现了由共形反常控制的应力张量三点函数⟨TTT⟩。

Conclusion: 本文在手征反常与共形反常之间建立了全新的双拷贝联系，这一发现不仅统一了看似无关的两类反常结构，还通过平直空间极限将CFT关联函数与高维散射振幅联系起来，为反常结构的研究提供了新的理论框架，并为后续在更高维度或更一般理论中探索类似双拷贝关系开辟了方向。

Abstract: We uncover a double-copy relation between chiral and conformal anomalies in four-dimensional conformal field theory. We show that this relation extends from local anomaly structures to complete momentum-space three-point correlators and, through their flat-space limits, to five-dimensional scattering amplitudes. In particular, the parity-odd current correlators associated with the chiral anomaly reduce, in the flat-space limit, to a universal amplitude generated by a Chern--Simons interaction. Squaring these anomalous current correlators reproduces the contribution to the stress-tensor three-point function $\langle TTT\rangle$, controlled by the conformal anomaly, thereby establishing a direct link between chiral- and conformal-anomaly structures.

### [130] [Information-Theoretic Black Hole Entropy I: Beyond the Area Law](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05795)

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Bekenstein-Hawking面积定律虽与热力学第一、第二定律一致，但与第三定律（零温极限下熵应消失或趋于普适常数）冲突。作者认为这一张力反映的是半经典面积定律的局限性，而非黑洞热力学的根本特征，因此需要修正熵公式。

Method: 通过放弃半经典面积定律在低温极限的适用性，构造一个与第三定律相容的熵公式。该熵可写成质量偏置Bernoulli分布与N个微观比特上的均匀分布之间的Kullback-Leibler散度。将热力学黑洞熵表示为相对于最大混合参考系综的相对熵（熵赤字），并引入普适质量标度M_0=√N·M_P作为黑洞质量的绝对上界。Bekenstein-Hawking面积律作为1/N展开的首项出现，亚领头项为经典面积律的有限信息修正。

Conclusion: 黑洞热力学熵可以信息论地解释为相对于最大混合参考系综的熵赤字（相对熵），Bekenstein-Hawking面积定律仅是1/N展开的前导近似，次领项提供了对经典面积律的有限信息修正，从而使黑洞熵与第三定律相容。

Abstract: Although the Bekenstein-Hawking area law is consistent with the first and second laws of black hole thermodynamics, it appears to be in conflict with the third law, which in the Nernst formulation states that the entropy should either vanish or approach a universal constant in the zero-temperature limit. We argue that this tension reflects a limitation of the semiclassical area law rather than a fundamental feature of black hole thermodynamics. On this basis, we obtain an entropy formula that is consistent with the third law, and approaches Bekenstein-Hawking entropy in the high-temperature limit. The resulting entropy admits a simple microscopic interpretation, and it can be written as the Kullback-Leibler divergence between a mass-biased Bernoulli distribution and the uniform distribution on N microscopic bits. From this perspective, the thermodynamic black hole entropy admits an information-theoretic representation as an entropy deficit, namely as the relative entropy between the black hole ensemble and a maximally mixed reference ensemble. The Bekenstein-Hawking area law then emerges as the leading term in an $1/N$ expansion, while a universal mass scale $M_0=\sqrt{N}\,M_P$, interpreted as an absolute upper bound on the black hole mass, controls the bias of the underlying microscopic ensemble. The subleading terms represent finite-information corrections to the classical area law.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>

Result: 在微扰线性阶，信息论熵与温度可被红外形变的广义相对论精确复现；该匹配自然产生一个小的正有效宇宙学常数（其大小与1/N相关）；Kerr-Newman扩展基于不可约质量，保留了视界面积联系和半经典极限，同时区分了几何极端性与与角动量和电荷无关的普适统计冻结端点。

Abstract: We investigate gravitational and Kerr-Newman extensions of an information-theoretic black-hole entropy that satisfies the Nernst formulation of the third law of thermodynamics. The entropy is identified with the Kullback-Leibler divergence between a mass-dependent Bernoulli ensemble and an unbiased reference ensemble, and therefore measures relative information rather than the logarithm of the number of black-hole microstates. We show perturbatively that its temperature and entropy can be reproduced by an infrared deformation of General Relativity. To linear order in the deformation couplings, the surface-gravity temperature and the Wald entropy agree with the information-theoretic results. In an explicit realization, the matching generates a positive effective cosmological term whose smallness is related to the large microscopic parameter $N$. We also propose an extension to Kerr-Newman black holes based on the irreducible mass. This construction preserves the connection with the horizon area and the semiclassical limit, while distinguishing geometrical extremality from the universal statistical-freezing endpoint, which is independent of angular momentum and charge.

</details>


### [132] [Emergent gravitational action from non-local $T\bar T$-like deformations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05913)
*Yun-Ze Li,Bo-Rui Li,Yu-Xiao Liu,Song He*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究了非局域T̄T型形变的量子场论在形变参数一阶下诱导的引力有效作用量。通过热核公式提取应力张量两点函数产生的局域几何项，应用于自由费米子、大规模Maxwell理论和二阶Yang-Mills理论。共形场论中存在由中心荷C_T固定的普适扇区，经正则化和重整化后，该扇区产生有限、方案无关的曲率不变量贡献。trace-trace形变则由Weyl反常决定接触项，并推导出一般最小非局域核对应的有限引力作用量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索非局域T̄T型形变在量子场论中诱导的引力有效作用量，理解共形数据（特别是中心荷C_T）如何决定涌现的几何响应，为诱导引力提供量子有效作用量的实现。

Method: 采用热核公式（heat-kernel formulation）从应力张量两点函数提取局域几何项；通过正则化与重整化处理发散；分别对自由费米子、大规模Maxwell理论、二阶Yang-Mills理论进行分析，并针对共形场论提取由中心荷C_T固定的普适贡献；进一步分析trace-trace形变的Weyl反常联系项。

Result: 结果表明各理论的贡献系数一般依赖于模型，但共形场论包含由中心荷C_T固定的普适扇区；经正则化重整化后，该扇区的贡献有限且方案无关，可组织为有限集合的曲率不变量。对于trace-trace形变，Weyl反常决定相关联系项，并给出了最小非局域核类的有限引力作用量。

Conclusion: 该研究提供了诱导引力的量子有效作用量实现，其中普适的共形数据（中心荷）决定了可计算的涌现几何响应的贡献，为理解形变量子场论与引力的关系建立了新的理论框架。

Abstract: We study the gravitational effective action induced, at first order in the deformation parameter, by non-local $T\bar T$-like deformations of quantum field theories. Using a heat-kernel formulation, we extract the local geometric terms generated by stress-tensor two-point functions, and apply the construction to free fermions, massive Maxwell theory, and second-order Yang-Mills theory. While the resulting coefficients are generally model dependent, conformal field theories contain a universal sector fixed by the central charge $C_T$. After regularization and renormalization, this sector yields finite, scheme-independent contributions organized into a finite set of curvature invariants. We further analyze trace-trace deformations, for which the relevant contact terms are determined by the Weyl anomaly, and derive the corresponding finite gravitational action for a general class of minimal non-local kernels. These results provide a quantum effective-action realization of induced gravity in which universal conformal data determine calculable contributions to the emergent geometric response.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在散射振幅研究中，色-动力学对偶性是连接规范理论与引力理论的重要工具。作者希望构建在更高圈阶（三圈）和更多外腿（五点）情况下满足该对偶的显式表示，并研究其紫外行为，以深化对两类理论之间关系的理解。

Method: 作者构造了一个显式满足色-动力学对偶性的三圈五点积分表示，利用双拷贝方法将其映射为N=8超引力的积分。在Dc=6临界维度下评估紫外极点，并将四维外部态的前因子替换为D维树级振幅构建的表达式，与开弦理论预言进行比较。

Result: 对于SYM振幅，使用D维树振幅替换后的表达式可以复现开弦理论对一般D维外部态的极点预测。但相应的引力表达式与弦论启发的表达式之间相差一个瞬变（evanescent）项。

Conclusion: 研究表明色-动力学对偶的双拷贝构造在推广到一般维度外部态时存在微妙之处：SYM侧能完美匹配弦预言，而引力侧存在瞬变项偏差，这暗示了维度推广过程中需要更精细的处理。

Abstract: We construct the complete full-color three-loop five-point integrand of N=4 super-Yang--Mills theory in a representation that manifestly satisfies color--kinematics duality. Its double copy gives the corresponding N=8 supergravity integrand. For four-dimensional external states, we evaluate the ultraviolet poles of both amplitudes in the critical dimension, Dc=6. Extending the external-state dependence to D dimensions is subtle. We consider a candidate replacement of the four-dimensional prefactors by expressions built from D-dimensional tree amplitudes. It reproduces the open-string prediction for the SYM pole with generic D-dimensional external states, whereas the corresponding gravity expression differs from the string-inspired one by an evanescent term.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的FLRW宇宙学研究通常将不同组分（如物质、辐射、暗能量）分别处理，且暴胀模型的解析求解依赖于慢滚动近似，其有效性在非slow-roll情形下值得商榷。作者希望建立一个统一的解析框架，使多组分宇宙学的动力学问题可积，并超越慢滚动近似精确计算宇宙学扰动观测量。

Result: 该框架实现了多组分FLRW宇宙学的解析可积性，构造出的暴胀模型与文献中已有模型性质相似且与观测兼容。通过超越慢滚动近似计算扰动观测量，结果与当前观测数据及基于慢滚动近似（作为特例）的理论预测进行了对比。

Conclusion: 该工作为多组分FLRW宇宙学及其扰动提供了一个统一的解析可积框架，将标准宇宙学、暴胀和暗能量纳入同一描述，并能在超越慢滚动近似的精度下计算可观测的宇宙学扰动量，为未来更精确的宇宙学模型检验提供了理论工具。

Abstract: We propose a novel analytic approach to the study of multi-component FLRW cosmologies and their perturbations. The dynamics is triggered by a single scalar field with a scalar potential codifying the energy density and pressure of the multi-component fluid. This description unifies standard Big Bang cosmologies, models of inflation and dark energy under a unique framework. The key to integrability is to express the scalar potential in terms of the Hubble function $H(φ)$ that plays the role of a fake superpotential, turning the dynamics into a first order problem, that may be analytically solved in a suitable time coordinate. In this framework, we propose integrable inflationary models with similar properties to the ones analysed in the literature and compatible with observations. Finally, we study scalar and tensor cosmological perturbations in each model by integrating Mukhanov-Sasaki equations via numerical and (semi-)analytic techniques. This allow us to compute the power spectrum, the spectral indices and the tensor-to-scalar ratio beyond the slow-roll approximation and compare our general results {against} the currently available observations and the theoretical predictions based on the slow-roll approximation.

</details>

Abstract: We study deformation of supersymmetric $t$-confining (truly confininig) gauge theories with small anomaly mediation of supersymmetry breaking. We identify breaking pattern of global symmetries in the theories. These results can be compared in priciple to lattice simulation of non-supersymmetric theories (except for one of the cases where the theory is pseudoreal and chiral).

</details>


### [136] [Pseudoreal AMSB: Troubling Tensions with Tumbling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06148)
*Bea Noether*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文分析了由异常媒介超对称破缺（AMSB）变形的伪实数约束N=1超对称规范理论。基于AMSB极限与非超对称极限属于同一普适类的猜想，作者发现这类非超对称约束伪实数规范理论的谱中不存在无质量粒子，这与近期tumbling机制的预期相矛盾。作者探讨了非标准tumbling是否可能调和两种图像，发现三个案例需要在排斥通道中凝聚，而一个案例无论吸引/排斥特性如何似乎都不可能。作者还简要评论了可能处于共形窗口的其他理论，发现其超对称版本与流向超共形不动点一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究伪实数约束超对称规范理论在AMSB变形下的非超对称极限行为，检验其粒子谱是否包含无质量粒子，并验证与tumbling机制的预测是否一致，从而加深对非超对称动力学（尤其质量谱与凝聚机制）的理解。

Method: 采用AMSB作为超对称破缺机制，借助AMSB与非超对称极限属于同一普适类的猜想，分析约束相中理论的光谱结构，并与tumbling（包括非标准tumbling）预测进行比较；同时考察位于共形窗口附近的额外理论，分析其超对称版本的流向是否指向超共形不动点。

Result: 该类别中的约束伪实数非超对称理论在其谱中没有无质量粒子，与tumbling的近期预期相矛盾。非标准tumbling仅能在三个案例中通过排斥通道凝聚来调和，而另一个案例中无论凝聚通道的吸引/排斥性质如何都无法调和。此外，可能处于共形窗口的理论的超对称版本与流向超共形不动点一致。

Conclusion: 在AMSB与非超对称极限属于同一普适类的假设下，约束伪实数规范理论不存在无质量粒子谱，表明tumbling预期需要修正；非标准tumbling的调和方案受限于凝聚通道的性质。共形窗口附近的理论则与超共形固定点行为相容。

Abstract: I analyze the pseudoreal confining $\mathcal{N}=1$ SUSY gauge theories deformed by anomaly mediated SUSY breaking (AMSB). Taking the conjecture that the AMSB and non-SUSY limits are in the same universality class, I find broadly that the non-SUSY confining pseudoreal gauge theories of this class have no massless particles in their spectra. This contradicts recent expectations from tumbling. I explore whether or not non-standard tumbling could reconcile the two pictures, and find that it would require condensation in repulsive channels in three of the cases, and that in one case it does not appear possible regardless of attractiveness/repulsiveness. I briefly comment on the additional theories that may be in the conformal window, and find that their SUSY versions are consistent with flowing to superconformal fixed points.

</details>


### [137] [Isomorphic Emergence of Lorentz and Gauge Symmetries--A Constructive Interpretation Based on Continuum Mechanics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06244)
