<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 洛伦兹对称性与规范对称性是现代物理学的数学基石，但它们最终的物理起源仍然未知。本文试图从构造性诠释的角度，为这两大对称结构提供一个统一的经典物理来源，而无需将其视为基本假设或依赖量子化过程。

Method: 以均匀各向同性弹性基底介质中横向波速为基准，采用约定主义同步方案，推导出闵可夫斯基时空与洛伦兹变换；对有SU(N)内禀对称性的波包，通过恒等不变性要求，论证介质自发产生规范场，得到同构于杨-米尔斯理论的规范结构；对非均匀介质分布，通过介质形变自由能的变分极值化，得到爱因斯坦型场方程。

Result: 三个核心结果：1）闵可夫斯基时空与洛伦兹变换作为有效坐标变换同构涌现，重新诠释了迈克尔逊-莫雷零结果；2）SU(N)规范群由介质支持的稳定简正模数唯一确定，规范结构同构于杨-米尔斯理论；3）非均匀介质分布同构涌现为弯曲时空几何，服从爱因斯坦型场方程。

Conclusion: 本文提供了一个统一的解释性框架，揭示了相对论和标准模型的数学结构背后共同的经典物理起源，表明两者均可从连续弹性基底介质中的波包激发动力学约束中同构涌现，为理解这两个核心理论框架的物理来源提供了新的统一视角。

Abstract: Lorentz symmetry and gauge symmetry constitute the mathematical cornerstones of modern physics, yet their ultimate physical origins remain elusive. From the standpoint of a constructive interpretation, this paper demonstrates that both symmetry structures can emerge isomorphically from a unified classical source: dynamical constraints on wave-packet excitations in a continuous elastic substrate medium (SM). Neither symmetry is posited as fundamental, nor does their emergence rely on quantization. Three core results are established. First, taking the transverse wave speed of a homogeneous, isotropic SM as the benchmark under a conventionalist synchronization scheme, Minkowski-type spacetime arises isomorphically, with Lorentz transformations as effective coordinate transformations between inertial frames. This reinterprets the Michelson--Morley null result: observers are composite wave-packet excitations, and medium-induced kinematic corrections are encoded in their measurement frameworks. Second, for wave packets endowed with $SU(N)$ intrinsic symmetry, the requirement of identity invariance drives the SM to spontaneously generate gauge fields. This requires invariant equivalence classes of intrinsic states under propagation, yielding gauge structures isomorphic to Yang-Mills theory. The gauge group $SU(N)$ is uniquely determined by the number of stable normal modes supported by the medium. Third, through the same conventionalist measurement protocols, inhomogeneous distributions of the SM emerge isomorphically as curved spacetime geometry, governed by Einstein-type field equations from variational extremization of the medium's deformation free energy. This paper advances a unified interpretive account of the physical origin underlying the mathematical structures of both Relativity and the Standard Model.

</details>


### [138] [Holographic entanglement entropy with conformal boundary conditions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06277)
*Elena Cáceres,Hare Krishna,Harita Palani Balaji,Vaishnavi Patil*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究了3维AdS引力中具有共形边界条件（固定边界度量的共形类和外在曲率K，但让Weyl模动力学化）的量子纠缠熵。通过推广Lewkowycz-Maldacena-Dong的replica构造，作者推导了对应的全息纠缠熵公式，证明涨落的Weyl模不贡献额外熵。全局AdS、旋转和非旋转BTZ几何中的显式计算表明：K依赖的全息纠缠熵由c_m=3ℓ/2G_N支配；高共形温度热态下熵由有效中心荷c_eff=3ℓ/(2G_N)·(Kℓ-√(K²ℓ²-4))/2支配。最后，从对偶边界理论（全息CFT耦合时间型Liouville理论并用类T\bar{T}边缘算符形变）直接计算得到S_EE=(c_eff/3)ln(2R sinφ₀/ε)，独立验证了c_eff，并澄清了真空态不是全局AdS的对偶态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在标准AdS/CFT中，全息纠缠熵由Ry-Takayanagi公式给出，但对于具有共形边界条件（Weyl模动力学化）的3维AdS引力，标准RT公式是否仍然适用是未解决的问题。作者旨在推广replica构造，推导该情况下的全息纠缠熵公式，并通过对偶边界理论的直接计算进行验证。

Method: 作者将Lewkowycz-Maldacena-Dong的replica构造推广到具有共形边界条件的3维AdS引力中，推导全息纠缠熵公式。对全局AdS、旋转和非旋转BTZ几何进行显式计算，并将结果与对偶边界理论（全息CFT耦合时间型Liouville理论+类T\bar{T}形变）的直接纠缠熵计算进行对比验证。

Result: (1) 动力学化Weyl模不贡献额外熵，全边界的熵为Bekenstein-Hawking熵，边界子区域的熵仍遵循RT公式（最小面积/4G_N）。(2) 对AdS₃中的区间，K依赖的纠缠熵由c_m=3ℓ/2G_N控制。(3) 高共形温度热态下，熵由有效中心荷c_eff=3ℓ/(2G_N)·(Kℓ-√(K²ℓ²-4))/2控制，与Cardy态密度一致。(4) 边界理论直接计算给出S_EE=(c_eff/3)ln(2R sinφ₀/ε)，独立验证了c_eff，且澄清真空态并非全局AdS的对偶态。

Conclusion: 共形边界条件下的3维AdS引力中，全息纠缠熵仍遵循RT公式，但有效中心荷由外在曲率K修正为c_eff，且与边界形变理论（Liouville+T\bar{T}）的预言完全一致。该工作建立了一个自洽的全息纠缠熵框架，并澄清了对偶态映射的细微问题。

Abstract: We study holographic entanglement entropy in 3-dimensional AdS gravity with conformal boundary conditions which fix the conformal class of the boundary metric and its extrinsic curvature, $K$, while leaving the Weyl mode dynamical. Extending Lewkowycz-Maldacena-Dong's replica construction, we derive the corresponding holographic entanglement entropy formula. We show that the fluctuating Weyl mode does not contribute additional entropy. The entropy of the full boundary is therefore the Bekenstein-Hawking entropy, and the entropy of a boundary subregion continues to obey the Ryu-Takayanagi prescription, namely, the area of a minimal surface divided by $4G_N$.
  We carry out explicit calculations for global AdS, rotating and non-rotating BTZ geometries. For an interval in AdS$_3$ we find that the $K$-dependent holographic entanglement entropy is governed by $c_m=\frac{3 \ell}{2 G_N}.$ For a thermal state at high conformal temperatures we find that, the entropy is governed by $c_{\rm eff}=\frac{3 \ell}{2 G_N} \frac{K \ell- \sqrt{K^2 \ell^2-4}}{2}$, the same effective central charge that governs the Cardy-like density of states, in agreement with previous results in the literature.
  Finally, we also compute the entanglement entropy directly from the conjectured dual boundary theory - a holographic CFT coupled with time-like Liouville theory and deformed by a marginal $T \bar{T}$ like operator - and find $S_{EE} =\frac{c_{\rm eff}}{3} \ln\!\left(\frac{2 R \sinφ_0}ε\right) .$ This result for the state with no operator insertions (vacuum state ), provides an independent boundary realization of $c_{\mathrm{eff}}$ while clarifying that this state is not the state dual to global AdS.

</details>


### [139] [GLSM monodromy on quantum period lattice of Calabi-Yau fourfold flops](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06280)
*Ban Lin*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文为特定的Calabi-Yau四折叠（CY4）翻折建立B-膜中心荷的积分基，并通过等级限制规则和规范线性sigma模型（GLSM）的窗口范畴导出其精确的幺正变换（monodromy）表达式。该幺正变换被解释为与将例外曲面收缩为圆锥奇点曲线相关的EZ扭转，并通过球面扭转的分解在六次CY4和格拉斯曼完全交集中加以阐释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究某种Calabi-Yau四折叠翻折的B-膜中心荷和幺正变换，目的在于理解这些几何变换下的不变结构，并建立其与GLSM窗口范畴和EZ扭转之间的联系。

Method: 利用等级限制规则和关联GLSM的窗口范畴来构造B-膜中心荷的积分基并导出幺正变换；将幺正变换解释为EZ扭转；最后通过球面扭转分解来阐释该EZ扭转，适用于六次CY4和格拉斯曼完全交集这两种分裂构型的族。

Result: 成功建立了B-膜中心荷的积分基，获得幺正变换的精确表达式，并将其标识为EZ扭转；在六次CY4和格拉斯曼完全交集上展示了EZ扭转可分解为球面扭转。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了CY4翻折B-膜拓扑不变量与其GLSM窗口范畴间的关系，并揭示了EZ扭转通过球面扭转分解的结构特性，为理解更高维Calabi-Yau流形上的D-膜范畴提供了新视角。

Abstract: We establish an integral basis for the B-brane central charges of certain Calabi-Yau fourfold flops and derive exact expressions for their monodromies using the grade restriction rule and window categories of the associated gauged linear sigma models. The monodromy is interpreted as an EZ twist associated with the contraction of an exceptional surface onto a curve of conifold singularities. We further illustrate a decomposition of the EZ twist into spherical twists for families of splitting configurations of the sixtic Calabi-Yau fourfold and a complete intersection in grassmannian.

</details>


### [140] [The Quantum Mechanics of Rare Events: From Quantum Walks to Stochastic Inflation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06319)
*Daniel Green,Kshitij Gupta,Akhil Premkumar*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文发展了量子系统中大偏差理论的实用方法，用于计算从量子漫步到宇宙学中的稀有涨落事件。作者首先分析了与热库耦合的非谐振子，展示了系统如何从统计涨落主导过渡到量子涨落主导；随后推广结果表明主导稀有涨落使测量诱导的相对熵最小化，为开放量子系统提供了热力学描述。最后应用于随机暴胀产生的量子漫步，发现德西特背景上长波长场密度矩阵的演化破坏了KMS对称性，产生不满足细致平衡的稳态密度矩阵。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典统计系统中大偏差原理已阐明半经典在稀有涨落描述中的作用，并为统计力学奠定了数学基础。然而量子系统的大偏差理论发展相对不足，而所有物理系统本质上是量子力学的，这导致在统计物理、宇宙学等领域中理解稀有涨落存在重大空白。

Method: 首先分析耦合热库的非谐振子情形，显式展示系统如何从统计性（热）涨落主导演化为量子涨落主导。然后将结果推广，证明主导稀有涨落最小化测量诱导的相对熵。最后将这些结果应用于随机暴胀中产生的量子漫步。

Result: 研究表明主导稀有涨落对应于最小化测量诱导相对熵的路径，为宽泛的开放量子系统提供了热力学描述。在宇宙学应用中，德西克背景上长波长场密度矩阵的演化破坏KMS对称性，产生不遵循细致平衡的稳态密度矩阵。

Conclusion: 该研究填补了量子系统大偏差理论的空白，为计算物理系统中（从量子漫步到宇宙学）的稀有事件提供了实用的理论框架，并揭示了开放量子系统在特定背景下会破坏热平衡对称性的重要物理特性。

Abstract: Rare fluctuations in physical systems depend on the detailed microphysics responsible for the fluctuations. In classical statistical systems, the large deviation principle has elucidated the role of semi-classics in describing this regime, and has simultaneously provided a the mathematical foundation of statistical mechanics. Large deviation theory for quantum system is considerably less developed. As all physical systems are fundamentally quantum mechanical, this leaves a major gap in our understanding of rare fluctuations relevant to statistical physics, cosmology, and more. In this paper, we develop the practical aspects of the theory of large deviations relevant for calculating rare events in physical systems from quantum walks to cosmology. We first analyze the case of the anharmonic oscillator coupled to a bath, showing explicitly how the system evolves from dominantly statistical (e.g. thermal) to quantum fluctuations. We then generalize these results, showing that the dominant rare fluctuations minimize the measurement-induced relative entropy. This perspective provides a thermodynamic description of a wide range of open quantum systems. We apply these results to random walks that arise in cosmology through stochastic inflation. We show that the evolution of the density matrix of long wavelength fields on a fixed de Sitter background breaks the KMS symmetry, giving rise to a stationary density matrix that does not respect detailed balance.

</details>


### [141] [Quantum Lifts of Noninteger Power Law Field Theories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06282)
*Jarah Evslin,Hengyuan Guo,Stefano Bolognesi*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种通过Hermite多项式展开并用参数β移动的质标度进行正规排序的方法，将非整数幂势能的经典场论提升为量子场论。当α>2且β足够大时，真空态可微扰展开为标准Fock态。该方法应用于σ=4 Pöschl-Teller模型（具有φ^{5/2}势），解决了其三体相互作用和kink最浅束缚模激发概率发散的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 具有非整数幂律势能的场论在许多领域有应用，但通常被认为只能作为有效模型而无法提升为量子场论。此外，σ=4 Pöschl-Teller模型的φ^{5/2}势在三阶导数处发散，导致三体相互作用在真空中发散，且kink的最浅束缚模延伸到真空深处使其被辐射激发的概率表观上发散。这些发散问题需要被解决。

Method: 将经典势能展开为Hermite多项式级数，并在由参数β移动的质量标度下进行正规排序（normal ordering）来定义量子提升。这样得到的量子哈密顿量在Fock空间中定义良好。作者证明了当α>2时，对于足够大的β，真空态可以用通常的Fock态进行微扰展开。

Result: 对于σ=4 Pöschl-Teller模型，发现当形变参数β取介子质量量级或更大时，足以驯服由于势能三阶导数发散导致的三点相互作用发散，以及kink最浅束缚模激发概率的发散问题。然而，该方法仍导致激发概率被β依赖的逆耦合分数幂所增强。

Conclusion: 通过β形变正规排序方法，可以严格地将具有非整数幂势能的经典场论（如φ^{5/2}）提升为量子场论，并解决其中的发散问题，尽管会引入β依赖的耦合增强效应。

Abstract: Field theories whose potentials have noninteger power laws $α$ have found many applications, but are often claimed to have no lift to quantum field theory except as effective models. We define quantum lifts by expanding the classical potential in Hermite polynomials and then normal ordering at a mass scale shifted by a parameter $β$. We find that when $α>2$, for sufficiently large $β$, the vacuum state can be perturbatively expanded in usual Fock states. We apply this to the following problem. The $σ=4$ Pöschl-Teller model has a $φ^{5/2}$ potential. As the third derivative of the potential diverges in each vacuum, one expects the three point interactions to diverge in the vacuum. The model's kink has three shape modes and the least bound mode extends so far into the vacuum that its probability of being excited by radiation apparently diverges. We show that a deformation $β$ of order the meson mass or larger is sufficient to tame this divergence, although it nonetheless results in an excitation probability which is enhanced by a $β$-dependent fractional power of the inverse coupling.

</details>


### [142] [A Tale of Two Compact Bosons](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06376)
*Christian Ferko,Vishnu Jejjala,Brandon Robinson*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文综述了神经网络场论（NN-FT）中局部高斯扇区与离散拓扑扇区相结合的方法，并将其应用于两个紧致玻色子系统：Berezinskii-Kosterlitz-Thouless（BKT）相变和玻色弦理论。结果表明，相同的局部神经采样器配合不同的离散拓扑数据，可以复现截然不同的紧致理论物理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经网络场论中，对于紧致理论而言，仅考虑局部高斯扇区是不够的，还必须对离散拓扑扇区求和。作者旨在展示如何通过混合连续/离散潜变量构造，使神经网络场论能够正确描述紧致理论中拓扑效应的作用。

Method: 采用混合连续/离散潜变量的构造方法。对于BKT相变，使用随机傅里叶特征自旋波采样器并补充显式的Coulomb气体涡旋扇区；对于玻色弦，用振子模式配以动量-绕数标签来构建。

Result: 该方法成功复现了BKT相变中的关键物理：临界温度以下的Gaussian临界线、临界温度以上的涡旋增殖、关联长度的本质奇异性以及Nelson-Kosterlitz跳跃；对玻色弦则复现了圆T-对偶性、Buscher变换、自对偶流代数增强以及T-fold toy模型。

Conclusion: 相同的局部神经采样器与不同的离散拓扑数据结合，可以产生物理上截然不同的紧致理论，说明离散拓扑扇区在神经网络场论中是必不可少且具有区分度的组成部分。

Abstract: Neural network field theory (NN-FT) defines a field theory by a network architecture together with a probability density on its latent variables. For compact theories the local Gaussian sector is only part of the story: one must also sum over discrete topological sectors. We review a mixed continuous/discrete latent-variable construction and apply it to two compact bosons. For the Berezinskii--Kosterlitz--Thouless transition, a random Fourier feature spin-wave sampler supplemented by an explicit Coulomb gas vortex sector reproduces the Gaussian critical line below $T_c$, vortex proliferation above $T_c$, the essential singularity of the correlation length, and the Nelson--Kosterlitz jump. For the bosonic string, oscillator modes augmented by momentum--winding labels reproduce circle T-duality, Buscher transformations on constant toroidal backgrounds, self-dual current algebra enhancement, and a toy T-fold. The common lesson is that the same local neural sampler, paired with different discrete topological data, yields physically distinct compact theories. These proceedings are based on arXiv:2604.02313.

</details>

### [143] [Ruled Minimal Surfaces in the 3-dimensional Solvable Group](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05344)
*Tiago F. Ferreira*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 该论文研究了三维可解李群（由一个实参数p控制，配备左不变度量）中的直纹极小曲面。当p≠0时，分类了所有正交直纹曲面并构造了新的极小曲面例子；当p=0时，参数化了所有直纹曲面并获得了额外的极小曲面例子。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究三维可解李群中的极小曲面，特别是直纹极小曲面的分类问题。这类群依赖于一个实参数p，对其几何结构的研究有助于理解不同参数下的曲面行为差异。

Method: 将可解李群按参数p进行分情况讨论。对于p≠0的情况，系统分类所有正交直纹曲面；对于p=0的退化情形，对所有直纹曲面进行参数化处理，并在此基础上构造极小曲面。

Result: 在p≠0时完成了正交直纹曲面的完整分类，并构造出新的极小曲面例子；在p=0时对所有直纹曲面给出了参数化描述，并额外获得了极小曲面的新例子。

Conclusion: 该工作为三维可解李群中的直纹极小曲面提供了完整分类，并针对不同参数情形分别给出了构造方法，丰富了对该族李群中极小曲面几何的理解。

Abstract: We study ruled minimal surfaces in a family of three-dimensional solvable Lie groups depending on a real parameter $p$ and endowed with left-invariant metrics. For $p\neq 0$, we classify all orthogonal ruled surfaces and construct new examples of minimal surfaces. For $p=0$, we parametrize all ruled surfaces and obtain additional examples of minimal surfaces.

</details>


### [144] [Deformable hypersurfaces of $\mathbb{H}^k\times \mathbb{S}^{n-k+1}$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05429)
*Miguel Ibieta Jimenez,Ruy Tojeiro*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文研究了双曲空间与球面的共形平坦黎曼乘积 $\mathbb{H}^k\times \mathbb{S}^{n-k+1}$（其中 $2\leq k\leq n-1$）中的可变形超曲面。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究黎曼乘积空间中可变形超曲面的分类问题，特别是共形平坦黎曼乘积流形中的情况。

Method: 对双曲空间和球面的乘积空间中的超曲面进行几何分析，探讨其在共形平坦条件下的可变形性。

Result: 对 $\mathbb{H}^k\times \mathbb{S}^{n-k+1}$ 中可变形超曲面给出了刻画。

Conclusion: 该研究推进了对共形平坦黎曼乘积空间中可变形超曲面的理解。

Abstract: We investigate the deformable hypersurfaces of the conformally flat Riemannian products $\mathbb{H}^k\times \mathbb{S}^{n-k+1}$, $2\leq k\leq n-1$, of hyperbolic space and a sphere with constant sectional curvatures $-1$ and $1$, respectively.

</details>


### [145] [Linearisation, splitting property and homotopy algebras](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05875)
*Seokbong Seol,Kai Wang*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文在分次余代数框架下研究形式向量场的线性化问题，证明形式向量场可线性化当且仅当满足某个分裂性质，并给出同构的显式递归构造。该准则简化了Basto-Gonçalves关于允许共振向量场的定理证明，建立了形式流形间态射的可线性化分裂判据，给出了Bandiera关于可线性化（即同伦阿贝尔）L∞[1]代数刻画的初等显式证明，并将此框架推广至A∞[1]代数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 形式向量场的线性化是微分几何与代数中的重要问题。已有研究（如Basto-Gonçalves定理、Bandiera对L∞[1]代数的刻画）依赖于不同的技术路线，缺乏统一的框架。本文旨在通过分次余代数的视角，提供一个统一且构造性的方法来处理形式向量场、形式流形间态射以及L∞[1]和A∞[1]代数的线性化问题。

Method: 本文采用分次余代数的形式化框架。核心方法包括：(1) 提出分裂性质（splitting property）作为形式向量场可线性化的充要条件；(2) 通过显式递归构造方式构建线性化同构；(3) 将该判据分别应用于形式流形间态射、L∞[1]代数和A∞[1]代数，逐一建立对应的分裂判据。

Result: 主要结果包括：(1) 形式向量场可线性化当且仅当满足分裂性质，且同构可显式递归构造；(2) 由此得到Basto-Gonçalves定理的精简证明；(3) 建立了形式流形间态射可线性化的分裂判据；(4) 给出了Bandiera关于L∞[1]代数线性化刻画的初等显式证明；(5) 将该判据推广至A∞[1]代数，证明其线性化可由类似分裂性质刻画。

Conclusion: 本文通过分次余代数框架统一了形式向量场、形式流形态射、L∞[1]代数和A∞[1]代数的线性化问题，证明了分裂性质在各类结构中是线性化的统一判据，并提供了构造性证明方法。该框架不仅简化了已有定理的证明，还为新结构（A∞[1]）的线性化刻画提供了途径。

Abstract: In this paper, we study the formal linearisation problem for vector fields in the framework of graded coalgebras. We prove that a formal vector field is linearisable if and only if it satisfies a splitting property, by providing an explicit recursive construction of the isomorphism that linearises it. This criterion yields a streamlined proof of Basto-Gonçalves' theorem on admissible resonant vector fields. We also establish a corresponding splitting criterion for morphisms of formal manifolds, proving that a morphism is linearisable if and only if it satisfies this property. Furthermore, we obtain an elementary and explicit proof of Bandiera's characterisation of linearisable (equivalently, homotopy abelian) $L_\infty[1]$ algebras. Finally, we extend this framework to $A_\infty[1]$ algebras, showing that their linearisability is similarly characterised by an analogous splitting property.

</details>


### [146] [A strict second-eigenvalue estimate for the scalar stability operator on surfaces in $\mathbb{RP}^{3}$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05458)
*Márcio Batista,Abraão Mendes*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文对闭浸入曲面 φ:Σ²↬ℝP³（不要求可定向或双侧）建立了尖锐的定量估计 λ₂(Δ+|σ|²+2) ≤ 2 - 2/Area∫H² + 4πχ(Σ)/Area，并证明了当 χ(Σ)≤0 时不等式严格成立（排除等号情形）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究浸入到实射影空间中的闭曲面上的谱几何问题，特别是含第二基本形式模长和平均曲率的拉普拉斯算子的第二特征值估计。在无定向性假设下推广已有的尖锐估计，并处理边界（等号）情形。

Method: 结合ℝP³到ℝS⁸的规范Veronese嵌入、共形测试函数方法、Obata型刚性论证以及ℝP³中闭平坦极小曲面的分类。

Result: 建立了尖锐的定量不等式，并证明在 χ(Σ)≤0 时严格不等式 λ₂(Δ+|σ|²+2)<2 成立，即在边界情形排除了等号。

Conclusion: 该结果给出了实射影空间中闭浸入曲面谱估计的完整刻画，填补了无定向/非双侧情形下等号分析的空白。

Abstract: Let $\varphi:Σ^2\looparrowright\mathbb{RP}^3$ be a closed immersed surface, with no orientability or, equivalently, two-sidedness assumptions. We establish the sharp quantitative estimate $$ λ_2(Δ+|σ|^2+2)\leq2-\frac{2}{\operatorname{Area}(Σ)}\int_ΣH^2dΣ+\frac{4πχ(Σ)}{\operatorname{Area}(Σ)}. $$ Our main contribution is the analysis of the borderline case, which rules out equality when $χ(Σ)\leq0$. More precisely, $$ λ_2(Δ+|σ|^2+2)<2\quad\text{whenever}\quadχ(Σ)\leq0. $$ The proof combines the canonical Veronese embedding of $\mathbb{RP}^3$ into $\mathbb{S}^8$, the conformal test function method, an Obata-type rigidity argument, and the classification of closed flat minimal surfaces in $\mathbb{RP}^3$.

</details>


### [147] [Compact balanced threefolds and LCK manifolds with constant holomorphic sectional curvature](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05598)
*Shuwen Chen,Junpeng Li*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文验证了紧致平衡三维流形在c≤0时满足Chern全纯截面曲率常数的猜想，并将该猜想推广到典范度量连接，对连通的紧致局部共形Kähler流形证明了此推广形式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Hermitian几何中有一个长期存在的猜想：具有常数Chern全纯截面曲率c的紧致Hermitian流形在c≠0时为Kähler流形，在c=0时为Chern平坦流形。该猜想在复二维情形已获证，但在高维情形仍普遍开放。本文旨在验证紧致平衡三维流形在c≤0时的情形，并将该猜想推广至典范度量连接。

Method: 受Huang和Wan方法的启发，作者利用典范度量连接的性质以及紧致平衡三维流形和局部共形Kähler流形的结构特征，对猜想进行验证和推广。对于平衡三维流形，结合c≤0的条件给出证明；对于典范度量连接，在连通的紧致局部共形Kähler流形上建立相应的推广结论。

Result: 证明了对紧致平衡三维流形在c≤0时，常数Chern全纯截面曲率的猜想成立。同时，将猜想推广到典范度量连接的情形，并在连通的紧致局部共形Kähler流形上成功建立了该推广结论。

Conclusion: 本文推进了Hermitian几何中常数Chern全纯截面曲率猜想在高维情形的研究，验证了平衡三维流形的部分情况，并将相关结论推广到典范度量连接和局部共形Kähler流形，为该猜想在更一般框架下的解决提供了新途径。

Abstract: A long-standing conjecture in Hermitian geometry says that a compact Hermitian manifold with constant Chern holomorphic sectional curvature $c$ is Kähler for $c\neq 0$ and Chern flat for $c=0$. Although the conjecture has been established in complex dimension two, it remains open in general in higher dimensions. We verify the conjecture for compact balanced threefolds when $c\leq 0$. For compact locally conformally Kähler manifolds, Chen, Chen, and Nie established the case $c\leq 0$, while Huang and Wan recently settled the remaining case. Inspired by the approach of Huang and Wan, we investigate a generalization of the conjecture for canonical metric connections and establish it for connected compact locally conformally Kähler manifolds.

</details>


### [148] [Equivalence of Lin--Lu--Yau curvature and 1/2-Ollivier curvature on weighted graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05939)
*Shiping Liu,Yunyan Yang*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文证明在加权图上，当惰性参数 p≥1/2 时，Lin–Lu–Yau 曲率与 p-Ollivier 曲率在缩放意义下等价，且阈值 1/2 是最优的。这推广了 Bourne 等人针对组合图的结果，并为 Bai 等人的 Lin–Lu–Yau 曲率流的全局存在性与唯一性提供了简洁证明。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前 Bourne 等人仅在组合图上证明了 Lin–Lu–Yau 曲率与 p-Ollivier 曲率的等价性，本文希望将该结果推广到更一般的加权图上，并由此为已有的曲率流结果提供更简洁的证明路径。

Method: 利用加权图上的测地距离与最优传输框架，分析 idleness 参数 p 对两类曲率的影响，证明在 p≥1/2 时两者在缩放意义下一致，并通过构造反例或边界情形说明 1/2 是最佳阈值。

Result: 在加权图上建立了 Lin–Lu–Yau 曲率与 p-Ollivier 曲率（p≥1/2）的等价关系，并证明了阈值 1/2 的尖锐性；进而基于该等价性给出 Bai 等人曲率流全局存在唯一性的简洁证明。

Conclusion: 该等价性不仅推广了现有组合图的结果至加权图，还揭示了两种曲率之间的内在联系，为曲率流的理论研究提供了新的工具和简洁的证明方法。

Abstract: In this note, we prove that, on weighted graphs, the Lin--Lu--Yau curvature coincides with the $p$-Ollivier curvature up to scaling whenever the idleness parameter $p\geq 1/2$. Moreover, the threshold $1/2$ is sharp. This extends an earlier result of Bourne et al. (Ollivier--Ricci idleness functions of graphs, SIAM J. Discrete Math., 32 (2018), no. 2, 1408-1424), where combinatorial graphs were considered. This observation yields a simple proof for the global existence and uniqueness of solutions of the Lin--Lu--Yau curvature flow in Bai et al. (Ollivier Ricci-flow on weighted graphs, Amer. J. Math. 146 (2024), 1723-1747).

</details>


### [149] [Dihedral Rigidity for Convex Polytopes by Smooth Approximation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06320)
*Yuchen Bi*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文基于Brendle的光滑逼近方法，证明了对凸多面体情形的Gromov二面角刚性猜想。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Gromov的二面角刚性猜想是几何与分析领域的重要猜想，验证其在凸多面体情形下的成立性是该领域的关键一步。

Method: 采用Brendle的光滑逼近方法，通过将凸多面体光滑化并将问题转化为光滑流形上的刚性分析来证明该猜想。

Result: 成功证明了Gromov二面角刚性猜想在凸多面体情形下成立。

Conclusion: 该工作为Gromov二面角刚性猜想在更一般情形下的证明提供了重要方向和方法支持。

Abstract: Following Brendle's smooth approximation approach, we give a proof of Gromov's dihedral rigidity conjecture for convex polytopes.

</details>
